# Chapter 1

**Golden-aligned annotated English revision — translation-golden-aligned-v1.0.0**

Based on the fixed root-tantra-v1.0.0. The earlier English draft is preserved unchanged. The comparison covers all 5,466 original anchors; every changed source string and added golden object is endnoted.

Adzom e-text means the supplied digital transcription, not the facsimile itself. Many golden changes recover the Adzom scan or separate its smaller notes. Footnotes retain the old wording and explain the selected treatment.

This is a complete source-difference reconciliation, not independent human certification of every inherited translation. Bracketed supplies, unresolved source forms, and provisional interpretations remain visible.

[All golden-source endnotes](../ENDNOTES.md) · [Source annotations](../SOURCE-ANNOTATIONS.md) · [Review scope and results](../REVIEW.md)

<a id="part-1"></a>
## Chapter 1

<a id="u00001"></a>

༅།[^G-U00001]

<a id="u00002"></a>

Here is the text named the Jewel-Producing Continuum of the Great All-Penetrating Word. [N-001](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-001)[^G-U00002]

<a id="u00003"></a>

༄༅།[^G-U00003]

<a id="u00004"></a>

Here is the Continuum of the Great All-Penetrating Word, Which Brings Forth Jewels. [N-T01](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t01)[^G-U00004]

<a id="u00005"></a>

gaṃgarbamatagā. [N-002](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-002) In the language of India:[^G-U00005]

<a id="u00006"></a>

Ratnakaraśabdamahāprasaṅgatantra nāma—‘The Jewel-Producing Continuum of the Great All-Penetrating Word.’ [N-001](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-001)[^G-U00006]

<a id="u00007"></a>

In the language of Tibet:

<a id="u00008"></a>

The Continuum of the Great All-Penetrating Word, Which Brings Forth Jewels.

<a id="u00009"></a>

To the Blessed One, the great naturally arisen, the supreme teacher,

<a id="u00010"></a>

glorious Samantabhadra, I pay homage.

<a id="a2000-c01-s08"></a>

*[Provisional source caption: Three lineages … beginning … [first line partly unread]. I bow to the teacher Samantabhadra, Father–Mother.]*[^G-A2000-C01-S08]

<a id="u00011"></a>

**[Source heading: Setting out the uncommon introductory setting.] [N-016](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-016)**

<a id="u00012"></a>

Thus did I hear at one time:

<a id="u00013"></a>

At the beginning of samsara and what is beyond it,

<a id="u00014"></a>

from the naturally arisen and unmade,[^G-U00014]

<a id="u00015"></a>

there appeared an aggregate of gathered elements:

<a id="u00016"></a>

earth, water, fire, and wind, the four elements;

<a id="u00017"></a>

at the center, wind-mind, causes and conditions.

<a id="u00018"></a>

The immeasurable palace, gathered and generated,

<a id="u00019"></a>

is not contrived by naturally arisen awareness. [N-003](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-003)

<a id="u00020"></a>

Where delusion is entirely pure,

<a id="u00021"></a>

the retinue of methods in which great bliss is gathered—

<a id="u00022"></a>

embodiments and primordial knowing, discerning knowing, and wind—

<a id="u00023"></a>

is indivisible, with no diverse objects of focus.

<a id="u00024"></a>

The teaching of great bliss through the methods of channels and winds

<a id="u00025"></a>

is explained in accordance with each one's experience. [N-003](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-003)

<a id="u00026"></a>

There is no past, future, or present;

<a id="u00027"></a>

there is no division at all in the indivisible.

<a id="u00028"></a>

All is naturally arising primordial knowing.

<a id="u00029"></a>

**[Source heading: Setting out the common introductory setting.] [N-016](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-016)**[^G-U00029]

<a id="u00030"></a>

Thus did I hear at one time:[^G-U00030]

<a id="u00031"></a>

Endowed with Joy, Heaped with Merit—

<a id="u00032"></a>

the earliest realm of beings. [N-004](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-004)

<a id="u00033"></a>

Earth and water, fire and wind—

<a id="u00034"></a>

the four great elements did not appear.

<a id="u00035"></a>

There was no form assembled from flesh and blood[^G-U00035]

<a id="u00036"></a>

in that realm of greatly pure self-appearance.

<a id="u00037"></a>

In the utterly beautiful and expanded

<a id="u00038"></a>

full number of a lotus's stamens,

<a id="u00039"></a>

at the central site endowed with a thousand [stamens], [N-004](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-004)[^G-U00039]

<a id="u00040"></a>

with all qualities complete,

<a id="u00041"></a>

a form embodiment ripened by four conditions—

<a id="u00042"></a>

a miraculously born body—was assumed

<a id="u00043"></a>

by the teacher, the buddha, the Blessed One,

<a id="u00044"></a>

who emanated in the beautiful form of an eight-year-old.

<a id="u00045"></a>

From the three gazes, the teacher's embodiment

<a id="u00046"></a>

appeared with inconceivable light.

<a id="u00047"></a>

The activity of emanation first arose.

<a id="u00048"></a>

In the initial setting for the arising of the continuum,[^G-U00048]

<a id="u00049"></a>

the seeds of the five were held. [N-005](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-005)[^G-U00049]

<a id="u00050"></a>

As for the miraculously born beings to be trained,

<a id="u00051"></a>

white in color and free of fleshly form,

<a id="u00052"></a>

with stainless embodiments of unimpeded penetration,

<a id="u00053"></a>

their coarse afflictions were self-purified;

<a id="u00054"></a>

they were not lying dormant,

<a id="u00055"></a>

and the two kinds of manifest circulation were absent. [N-005](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-005)

<a id="u00056"></a>

The five faculties were self-clear;

<a id="u00057"></a>

their distinguishing marks subsided within.

<a id="u00058"></a>

Coming and going dependent on breath were exhausted.

<a id="u00059"></a>

Having taken up abiding in the open sky,

<a id="u00060"></a>

they turned about and rose, back and forth.

<a id="u00061"></a>

Their walking, standing, and lying down,

<a id="u00062"></a>

their movements of stirring and running,

<a id="u00063"></a>

were nowhere other than in the open sky itself.

<a id="u00064"></a>

Light arose from themselves and appeared;

<a id="u00065"></a>

the light of the sun, moon, and so forth did not appear.

<a id="u00066"></a>

Unclothed, with a gentle manner,

<a id="u00067"></a>

beautiful and graceful,

<a id="u00068"></a>

at ease, with consciousness unimpaired,

<a id="u00069"></a>

as though abiding in the first meditative absorption,

<a id="u00070"></a>

they were free of manifest doing and agency.

<a id="u00071"></a>

Like the transformative agency of formless karma,

<a id="u00072"></a>

they did not appear through constituents together with deterioration. [N-006](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-006)

<a id="u00073"></a>

In particular, from the lotus of the core,

<a id="u00074"></a>

the forms, one thousand and one in number,

<a id="u00075"></a>

like rainbows and illusions,

<a id="u00076"></a>

were the form embodiments of the buddhas.

<a id="u00077"></a>

Distinct among them was the teacher's form,

<a id="u00078"></a>

with immeasurable patterned rays of light.

<a id="u00079"></a>

I, the divine youth Joy-Maker,

<a id="u00080"></a>

had assumed a body complete in mastery.[^G-U00080]

<a id="u00081"></a>

In the abode of the light rays of the teacher's speech,

<a id="u00082"></a>

I found bliss through intrinsic nature. [N-T05](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t05)

<a id="u00083"></a>

Then, through the blessing of his activity,

<a id="u00084"></a>

sixty-four stars of light

<a id="u00085"></a>

appeared as an array of forms in the open sky,

<a id="u00086"></a>

without having been made by anyone.

<a id="u00087"></a>

In particular, the seventeen utmost refinements, [N-007](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-007)

<a id="u00088"></a>

for the completion of the sacred teaching—

<a id="u00089"></a>

the actual teaching of embodiment, speech, and awakened mind, [N-T04](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t04)

<a id="u00090"></a>

together with vajras and embodied forms—

<a id="u00091"></a>

had not been made by anyone.

<a id="u00092"></a>

As self-appearance from the primordial outset,[^G-U00092]

<a id="u00093"></a>

in accordance with each one's karma and fortune,[^G-U00093]

<a id="u00094"></a>

they appeared in the very golden core,

<a id="u00095"></a>

not simultaneously to everyone.

<a id="u00096"></a>

Thus, like the heart, they are secret.

<a id="u00097"></a>

Since the ultimate supreme accomplishments

<a id="u00098"></a>

do not involve exertion and effort,

<a id="u00099"></a>

whoever encounters this is certain to be liberated.

<a id="u00100"></a>

Thus the Great Perfection is free from doing and effort. [N-T08](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t08)

<a id="u00101"></a>

It does not arise from other exertion.

<a id="u00102"></a>

Buddhas beyond expression

<a id="u00103"></a>

have paid homage from the primordial outset

<a id="u00104"></a>

to the dharma embodiment, in which it was certainly transmitted.

<a id="u00105"></a>

To make it appear to those with the appropriate karma,[^G-U00105]

<a id="u00106"></a>

the seed of emanation arises.

<a id="u00107"></a>

From that actual teaching,

<a id="u00108"></a>

this magical display of reflected forms,

<a id="u00109"></a>

which no one can express,

<a id="u00110"></a>

[was proclaimed] from the sky, undivided from space: [N-008](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-008)

<a id="u00111"></a>

even before all phrases,

<a id="u00112"></a>

from still above the ‘nine, two, and four,’ [N-008](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-008)

<a id="u00113"></a>

it was proclaimed in the melody of Great Brahmā.

<a id="u00114"></a>

Then, from the space of the nature of phenomena,

<a id="u00115"></a>

through the speech of the Great All-Pervader,

<a id="u00116"></a>

blessing displayed it as its own sound. [N-T03](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t03)

<a id="u00117"></a>

Then, from space free of directions,

<a id="u00118"></a>

from the melodious sound of the kalaviṅka,

<a id="u00119"></a>

which gathers the quintessence of all teachings,

<a id="u00120"></a>

this very king of continua,

<a id="u00121"></a>

even before all phrases,

<a id="u00122"></a>

was drawn from three hundred and sixty

<a id="u00123"></a>

sounds and spoken by the teacher.

<a id="u00124"></a>

Then, the sounds of the individual elements,

<a id="u00125"></a>

from the agencies of heat and cold—

<a id="u00126"></a>

eight thousand individual melodious sounds—[^G-U00126]

<a id="u00127"></a>

appeared blessed by the teacher.

<a id="u00128"></a>

In particular, in the speech that distinguishes,

<a id="u00129"></a>

the Ground from which words and phrases arise [N-T09](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t09)

<a id="u00130"></a>

was taught in sixty limbs by the teacher,

<a id="u00131"></a>

who spoke to the gathered retinue of self-appearance.

<a id="u00132"></a>

What was the enlightened intent of this?

<a id="u00133"></a>

The manner of speaking had three enlightened intents:

<a id="u00134"></a>

antidote, condensation, and the meaning for the faculties. [N-009](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-009)

<a id="u00135"></a>

The phrases were condensed and made to appear.

<a id="u00136"></a>

No other phrases expressing their subject matter

<a id="u00137"></a>

are spoken by the elements of the three times.

<a id="u00138"></a>

Words and phrases were definitively condensed into names;

<a id="u00139"></a>

even the kalaviṅka's sounds,

<a id="u00140"></a>

for drawing in, engaging, and being pleasing,

<a id="u00141"></a>

were condensed from twelve clarifiers of word and meaning

<a id="u00142"></a>

into four or five expressive phrases. [N-010](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-010)

<a id="u00143"></a>

Definitively condensed into the teacher's speech,

<a id="u00144"></a>

there were a thousand and five in Brahmā's melody,

<a id="u00145"></a>

a hundred and three definite expressive forms,

<a id="u00146"></a>

definitively condensed into thirty lengtheners.

<a id="u00147"></a>

There were sixteen clarifiers among them,

<a id="u00148"></a>

sixty-two limbs of ‘realization-clearing,’

<a id="u00149"></a>

and forty-two limbs free from conceptual elaborations. [N-010](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-010)

<a id="u00150"></a>

The two classes specially condensed

<a id="u00151"></a>

are the foundation of words and phrases.

<a id="u00152"></a>

What is the enlightened intent of that?

<a id="u00153"></a>

Through each one's karmic magic,

<a id="u00154"></a>

the roots of training—‘fourteen, two’— [N-011](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-011)

<a id="u00155"></a>

those skilled in designs

<a id="u00156"></a>

bring forth emanations and so on[^G-U00156]

<a id="u00157"></a>

and know the meanings of constant display. [N-011](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-011)

<a id="u00158"></a>

In the magical display of what was thus spoken,

<a id="u00159"></a>

even before all continua,

<a id="u00160"></a>

from the root words and phrases of the All-Penetrating,

<a id="u00161"></a>

all certainly arose and proliferated as teaching.

<a id="u00162"></a>

The sources of the sacred teaching were established;

<a id="u00163"></a>

by its being spoken, samsara is made to subside.

<a id="u00164"></a>

Its qualities are beyond thought.

<a id="u00165"></a>

At the beginning of the emanated teaching,

<a id="u00166"></a>

it increased through the arising of this.

<a id="u00167"></a>

At the outset, when all bodies were miraculously born,

<a id="u00168"></a>

in the age of immeasurable lifespans,

<a id="u00169"></a>

for miraculously born beings,

<a id="u00170"></a>

intrinsic nature was undivided and perfectly pure.

<a id="u00171"></a>

All was expressed as indivisible in essence;

<a id="u00172"></a>

words and phrases were bliss-awareness.

<a id="u00173"></a>

The dharma embodiment too was without cause;

<a id="u00174"></a>

the complete enjoyment embodiment spread out, all-pervading; [N-T07](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t07)

<a id="u00175"></a>

the Ground for the emanation embodiment's elaboration was complete.

<a id="u00176"></a>

Without division, they entered balanced union,

<a id="u00177"></a>

and in the Great Perfection of intrinsic nature

<a id="u00178"></a>

abided together in a single maṇḍala.

<a id="u00179"></a>

Thus they were indivisible, of a single condition.[^G-U00179]

<a id="u00180"></a>

Then, one with this and not separate, [N-012](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-012)[^G-U00180]

<a id="u00181"></a>

From among the retinue whose intrinsic nature was pure,

<a id="u00182"></a>

the Lord of Gods made this request:

<a id="u00183"></a>

‘Ema! Listen, listen, Blessed One!

<a id="u00184"></a>

This sacred appearance,[^G-U00184]

<a id="u00185"></a>

this inconceivable gathering—

<a id="u00186"></a>

the intrinsic nature of its phrases is thus:

<a id="u00187"></a>

What is the enlightened intent of the four elements? [N-013](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-013)[^G-U00187]

<a id="u00188"></a>

How are all the meanings of the limbs pursued?

<a id="u00189"></a>

What is the meaning of its enlightened intent?

<a id="u00190"></a>

How extensive is the expression of words?

<a id="u00191"></a>

How many sites of penetration are there?

<a id="u00192"></a>

What are transformation and its agents? [N-013](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-013) [N-T20](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t20)[^G-U00192]

<a id="u00193"></a>

What clearly expresses the chapters?

<a id="u00194"></a>

Who desires this perfection?

<a id="u00195"></a>

What is the meaning of the arising of the introductory setting?

<a id="u00196"></a>

How many remaining teachings are there?

<a id="u00197"></a>

From what does the cause of destruction arise?[^G-U00197]

<a id="u00198"></a>

After destruction, from where does abiding arise?

<a id="u00199"></a>

Who maintains it in the interval?

<a id="u00200"></a>

Finally, into what does this very thing turn?

<a id="u00201"></a>

What are the body and its limbs like?

<a id="u00202"></a>

What do the elements desire?

<a id="u00203"></a>

What are the successive ages of sentient beings?

<a id="u00204"></a>

What are the outer, the inner, and the meaning?

<a id="u00205"></a>

What are the transitions of time?

<a id="u00206"></a>

What are days, months, and years?

<a id="u00207"></a>

What sustains life and vitality?

<a id="u00208"></a>

What is the endpoint of dispersal, penetration, and balance? [N-014](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-014)

<a id="u00209"></a>

What cuts the continuum of samsara's path?

<a id="u00210"></a>

What accomplishes the teacher's qualities?

<a id="u00211"></a>

How should one train in the essence of the three embodiments?

<a id="u00212"></a>

What is the scope of common activity?

<a id="u00213"></a>

How are the individual embodiments accomplished?

<a id="u00214"></a>

What are sleep and dreams like?

<a id="u00215"></a>

To what should cultivation for taming ordinary mind be applied?

<a id="u00216"></a>

What is the enlightened intent of what is called wind?

<a id="u00217"></a>

How many qualities do the channels have?

<a id="u00218"></a>

Likewise, what are the aspects of the spheres?

<a id="u00219"></a>

What is the scope of the ḍākinīs' activity?

<a id="u00220"></a>

What is the activity belonging to the aspects of transference?

<a id="u00221"></a>

Of what is dependent connection the cause?

<a id="u00222"></a>

By what are illness and suffering liberated?

<a id="u00223"></a>

What is the manner of the elements' transference?

<a id="u00224"></a>

Which elements belong to the crucial points?

<a id="u00225"></a>

What limbs of activity belong to calculation and conjunction?

<a id="u00226"></a>

By what is the bliss of the elements accomplished?

<a id="u00227"></a>

By what is the activity of relocation carried out?

<a id="u00228"></a>

What distinguishes the aware from the insentient?

<a id="u00229"></a>

What method reverses the crucial points themselves?

<a id="u00230"></a>

By what is the enlightened intent of the tenets established?

<a id="u00231"></a>

What is the enlightened intent concerning the ultimate?

<a id="u00232"></a>

How many stages of accomplishment are there?

<a id="u00233"></a>

How do the natural states abide?

<a id="u00234"></a>

How should the reckonings of delusion be held?

<a id="u00235"></a>

By what are elaborations about objects of knowledge cut?

<a id="u00236"></a>

What is the method of relying on a lama?

<a id="u00237"></a>

How are the outer elemental signs discerned?

<a id="u00238"></a>

What are the junctures of time and teaching?

<a id="u00239"></a>

What is the rite of conferring empowerment?

<a id="u00240"></a>

What are the pledges and vows like?

<a id="u00241"></a>

What subject matter is taught through phrases?

<a id="u00242"></a>

Beyond phrases, by what are elaborations cut?

<a id="u00243"></a>

How is this applied to the crucial point of direct perception?

<a id="u00244"></a>

By what are samsara and nirvana separated? [N-T11](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t11)

<a id="u00245"></a>

How many aspects of activity are there in retaining wind?

<a id="u00246"></a>

How many stages are there in training to hold ordinary mind? [N-T15](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t15)

<a id="u00247"></a>

What are the outer, inner, and secret signs of death?

<a id="u00248"></a>

By what is viewing brought to its endpoint?

<a id="u00249"></a>

What are the life-pillars of cultivation?

<a id="u00250"></a>

How is activity applied?

<a id="u00251"></a>

How extensive is the site of attaining the result?

<a id="u00252"></a>

By what is samsara's flowing stream cut?[^G-U00252]

<a id="u00253"></a>

What is the taking of nourishment from vital essences? [N-T16](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t16)

<a id="u00254"></a>

By what is clothing accomplished?

<a id="u00255"></a>

How is the flow of the elements cut? [N-T17](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t17)

<a id="u00256"></a>

By what are the tastes of the four balanced?

<a id="u00257"></a>

How do qualities arise in the treasury?

<a id="u00258"></a>

By what are the noble ones' miraculous powers accomplished?

<a id="u00259"></a>

How should one enjoy the treasures?

<a id="u00260"></a>

By what are the five Grounds brought under control?

<a id="u00261"></a>

I have not previously known these things.

<a id="u00262"></a>

Lord of appearance, speak to me!

<a id="u00263"></a>

By clearly distinguishing their sequence for me,

<a id="u00264"></a>

in the times to come,

<a id="u00265"></a>

all who have faith in and aspire to this

<a id="u00266"></a>

will come to trust this Great Perfection.

<a id="u00267"></a>

The simple, free from doing, will find great bliss. [N-019](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-019)

<a id="u00268"></a>

When samsara is exhausted to its very end,

<a id="u00269"></a>

they will find the entrance to the path beyond sorrow.

<a id="u00270"></a>

Therefore, whatever I have asked,

<a id="u00271"></a>

clearly distinguish its sequence and speak!’

<a id="u00272"></a>

Then, the all-pervading lord, the hero, [N-020](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-020)[^G-U00272]

<a id="u00273"></a>

though words and phrases were not proclaimed as names,

<a id="u00274"></a>

in space whose intrinsic nature is uncontrived,

<a id="u00275"></a>

without being written or arranged,

<a id="u00276"></a>

possessed a melody with sixty limbs.

<a id="u00277"></a>

Like a mere unwritten design,

<a id="u00278"></a>

to the faculties endowed with luster,

<a id="u00279"></a>

it certainly appeared in direct perception.

<a id="u00280"></a>

Also in the sound of Great Brahmā:

<a id="u00281"></a>

‘Indra, sovereign of the gods, listen!

<a id="u00282"></a>

Having thoroughly disciplined body, speech, and mental faculty, [N-021](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-021)

<a id="u00283"></a>

abandon wrong activity and thinking.

<a id="u00284"></a>

Having purified the faculties and so forth,

<a id="u00285"></a>

listen with ordinary mind from which stains have been abandoned.

<a id="u00286"></a>

Ema! How wonderful and astonishing!

<a id="u00287"></a>

This, not spoken by the buddhas,

<a id="u00288"></a>

surpasses other continua.

<a id="u00289"></a>

So that there may be no return to abiding in the three realms,

<a id="u00290"></a>

this most secret, definitive command,

<a id="u00291"></a>

for increasing the quintessence of the teaching,

<a id="u00292"></a>

is the very highest summit of the vehicles.

<a id="u00293"></a>

It gathers, through meaning, the mirror of the teachings;

<a id="u00294"></a>

it is the commentary on the command, the heart of the pith instructions,

<a id="u00295"></a>

the great perfection whose intrinsic nature is secret,

<a id="u00296"></a>

the heart-commentary, the eye of authoritative transmission,

<a id="u00297"></a>

the most secret key, the inner depth of the view,

<a id="u00298"></a>

the training ground of activity, the crucial point of cultivation,

<a id="u00299"></a>

the cutter of the succession of wombs, the sweeper of the lower destinies. [N-022](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-022)

<a id="u00300"></a>

I will explain the supreme secret continuum;

<a id="u00301"></a>

retinue who also hold the vajra, listen!

<a id="u00302"></a>

In the phrases I elaborate,

<a id="u00303"></a>

there is not a particle of the dust of delusion.

<a id="u00304"></a>

The teachings taught from this point onward

<a id="u00305"></a>

are certain to be accomplished and to bring forth meaning;

<a id="u00306"></a>

without doubt they yield the result.

<a id="u00307"></a>

Therefore I teach the quintessence of the teaching.

<a id="u00308"></a>

Listen, listen, Joy-Maker—take hold of this!’

<a id="u00309"></a>

As the phrases of the questions were clearly distinguished,

<a id="u00310"></a>

from the All-Pervader's voice,

<a id="u00311"></a>

emanations of letters such as these,

<a id="u00312"></a>

in space whose intrinsic nature is uncontrived,

<a id="u00313"></a>

unarranged phrases, naturally arisen,

<a id="u00314"></a>

appeared in the manner of questions and replies.

<a id="u00315"></a>

Accomplished through the settling of the basic condition, [N-023](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-023)

<a id="u00316"></a>

they appeared thus in the Ground from which phrases arise:[^G-U00316]

<a id="u00317"></a>

*Source annotation moved to endnotes; no root verse at U00317.*[^G-U00317]

<a id="u00318"></a>

*Source annotation moved to endnotes; no root verse at U00318.*[^G-U00318]

<a id="scan-ch1-layer-00318"></a>

**[Source heading: First reply.]**[^G-SCAN-CH1-LAYER-00318]

<a id="u00319"></a>

The purposes of the elements are as follows:[^G-U00319]

<a id="u00320"></a>

Water gathers the refined part

<a id="u00321"></a>

and performs the activity of separating the dregs

<a id="u00322"></a>

in each individual body.

<a id="u00323"></a>

Recognize its purpose, measure, and number.

<a id="u00324"></a>

Earth supports the foundation

<a id="u00325"></a>

and performs the activities of forming and establishing.

<a id="u00326"></a>

Through ripening, it generates channels and so forth.

<a id="u00327"></a>

Know its purpose in the conventional sphere.

<a id="u00328"></a>

Fire clears away the elements, [N-025](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-025)[^G-U00328]

<a id="u00329"></a>

and performs the activities of ripening and clarifying.

<a id="u00330"></a>

By blazing and spreading, it generates the eyes and so forth.

<a id="u00331"></a>

It makes one know its purpose in the forms of warmth.

<a id="u00332"></a>

Wind supports and lifts,

<a id="u00333"></a>

and performs the activities of stirring and swaying.

<a id="u00334"></a>

By vibrating and fluttering, it sustains the body.

<a id="u00335"></a>

Know its purpose in the abiding of ordinary mind.

<a id="u00336"></a>

The great maṇḍala of these four elements

<a id="u00337"></a>

acts as the cause of the formation of the body and so forth.

<a id="u00338"></a>

In the interval, it brings about abiding and maintenance;

<a id="u00339"></a>

at the end, it performs all the activities of destruction.

<a id="u00340"></a>

The great elements, having a single cause,

<a id="u00341"></a>

appear as the masters of diverse functions.

<a id="u00342"></a>

Thus their purposes, for self and other,

<a id="u00343"></a>

are skilled in the meaning of completing what is to be done.[^G-U00343]

<a id="u00344"></a>

**[Source heading: Second reply.]**[^G-U00344]

<a id="u00345"></a>

The meaning of the limbs is as follows:[^G-U00345]

<a id="u00346"></a>

The clear sequence of phrases expressing

<a id="u00347"></a>

the knowing of the buddhas

<a id="u00348"></a>

is explained through the presentation of the title and through the text,

<a id="u00349"></a>

so that conceptual minds of the gradual and simultaneous types

<a id="u00350"></a>

may realize it. [N-T18](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t18)

<a id="u00351"></a>

The text is taught in two aspects:

<a id="u00352"></a>

the establishment of the body itself,

<a id="u00353"></a>

and its extensive division through the meanings of the limbs.

<a id="u00354"></a>

The body is the two aspects of the introductory setting.

<a id="u00355"></a>

In order to surpass others in particular,

<a id="u00356"></a>

it is applied to the uncommon body.

<a id="u00357"></a>

In order to accord in approach with other vehicles,

<a id="u00358"></a>

the common introductory setting

<a id="u00359"></a>

is taught to inspire trust

<a id="u00360"></a>

in the compiler's own retinue.

<a id="u00361"></a>

Arising from the Ground of training as that which trains,

<a id="u00362"></a>

it makes the teaching abide.

<a id="u00363"></a>

The limbs are examples and the meanings of phrases.

<a id="u00364"></a>

Through the six chapters of the text itself,

<a id="u00365"></a>

the meanings of the phrases, just as they abide,

<a id="u00366"></a>

are shown like opening the interior of a body

<a id="u00367"></a>

and revealing its solid and hollow organs and so forth.

<a id="u00368"></a>

The meanings of the phrases have two aspects,

<a id="u00369"></a>

spoken as questions and replies.

<a id="u00370"></a>

The replies are explained in two ways: briefly and extensively.

<a id="u00371"></a>

To suit the inclinations of the individuals who are to be trained,

<a id="u00372"></a>

diverse elaborations are the meaning.[^G-U00372]

<a id="u00373"></a>

**[Source heading: Third reply.]**

<a id="u00374"></a>

Ema! In the definite arising of the continua,

<a id="u00375"></a>

there are two aspects of enlightened intent.

<a id="u00376"></a>

For those whose conceptual mind concerns awareness as self-appearance, [N-026](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-026)

<a id="u00377"></a>

the meaning of the continuum's title is distinguished.

<a id="u00378"></a>

Through the three—sign, word, and meaning—

<a id="u00379"></a>

direct perception of the nature of phenomena is made the path.

<a id="u00380"></a>

Once the flow of samsara in the three realms has been cut,

<a id="u00381"></a>

because there is no turning back again and again,

<a id="u00382"></a>

one knows the defining characteristic of exhaustion.

<a id="u00383"></a>

For conceptual minds that focus on objects,

<a id="u00384"></a>

the texts of the continua are distinguished.

<a id="u00385"></a>

The definitive crucial point corresponding to each inclination

<a id="u00386"></a>

is applied to each one individually.

<a id="u00387"></a>

If it is fully realized without separation,

<a id="u00388"></a>

one does not abide in samsara.

<a id="u00389"></a>

People who delight in elaboration itself,

<a id="u00390"></a>

having trained in diverse methods,

<a id="u00391"></a>

also take pride in their respective accomplishments. [N-026](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-026)

<a id="u00392"></a>

The pith instructions on the meaning of the crucial points of enlightened intent

<a id="u00393"></a>

should be known in their respective contexts.

<a id="u00394"></a>

**[Source heading: Fourth reply.]**

<a id="u00395"></a>

Through this very king of continua,

<a id="u00396"></a>

the varieties of its definitive title

<a id="u00397"></a>

are taught; as for the pith instructions on the meaning of perfection,

<a id="u00398"></a>

the phrases alone are examined and distinguished with certainty.

<a id="u00399"></a>

The syllables, too, in accord with their meanings,

<a id="u00400"></a>

are brought to the face of meaning in this way:

<a id="u00401"></a>

This manner of expressing sound [N-T03](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t03)

<a id="u00402"></a>

has not been explained in other continua.

<a id="u00403"></a>

Ema! How greatly wonderful!

<a id="u00404"></a>

I will elaborate it; listen to me.

<a id="u00405"></a>

The occasions of sound have five aspects:

<a id="u00406"></a>

Brahmā, the All-Pervader, the kalaviṅka,

<a id="u00407"></a>

the sounds of the elements, and the teacher's speech.

<a id="u00408"></a>

Through these five, in the phenomena that arise,

<a id="u00409"></a>

one engages freely in great play.

<a id="u00410"></a>

For the five sites of Brahmā,

<a id="u00411"></a>

there are two hundred for each,

<a id="u00412"></a>

and one acting as a further site for each.

<a id="u00413"></a>

Since there are a thousand and five further sites,

<a id="u00414"></a>

merely by knowing this

<a id="u00415"></a>

one is brought into contact with the Blessed One.

<a id="u00416"></a>

As for the All-Pervader, from further sites,

<a id="u00417"></a>

by two and five and ‘six, three,’ [N-027](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-027)

<a id="u00418"></a>

the cause of the activity of parting the side-locks—

<a id="u00419"></a>

when the grouping with its marks is complete,

<a id="u00420"></a>

there are a hundred for each of these. [N-027](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-027)

<a id="u00421"></a>

From the aspects of the magic that definitely distinguishes,

<a id="u00422"></a>

if one knows how to express each of those numbers,

<a id="u00423"></a>

higher knowing is accomplished in one's continuum.

<a id="u00424"></a>

Through the melodious sounds of the kalaviṅka,

<a id="u00425"></a>

there are a hundred and twenty for the body,

<a id="u00426"></a>

and likewise for speech and ordinary mind—

<a id="u00427"></a>

the same number as above is spoken in definite expression.

<a id="u00428"></a>

When the worldly gods,

<a id="u00429"></a>

likewise the gods beyond the world,

<a id="u00430"></a>

and the ḍākinīs gather,

<a id="u00431"></a>

if one knows the distinctions of their sounds,

<a id="u00432"></a>

one knows their languages.

<a id="u00433"></a>

When one definitely engages in the six kinds of higher knowing, [N-T19](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t19)

<a id="u00434"></a>

one knows the meanings of the Bliss-Gone One's commands.

<a id="u00435"></a>

Through the number of verses,

<a id="u00436"></a>

one is definitively connected with the individual sites.

<a id="u00437"></a>

Then, in the sound of water too,

<a id="u00438"></a>

eloquent speech is inconceivable.

<a id="u00439"></a>

For the skilled, the rite is complete.

<a id="u00440"></a>

As for the word and meaning of the elements,

<a id="u00441"></a>

through the hero and the hero-goer, [N-028](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-028)

<a id="u00442"></a>

in each of the four—earth, water, fire, and wind—

<a id="u00443"></a>

there are two apiece.

<a id="u00444"></a>

Since the neutral one is empty,

<a id="u00445"></a>

from the distinction between male and female

<a id="u00446"></a>

there are the causal agencies of heat and cold.

<a id="u00447"></a>

There are two thousand for each;

<a id="u00448"></a>

if a yogin knows how to express

<a id="u00449"></a>

the sounds of expression and designation,

<a id="u00450"></a>

then in the languages of all of these,

<a id="u00451"></a>

one delights in listening with ease and well-being.

<a id="u00452"></a>

Whoever has definitely mastered this

<a id="u00453"></a>

is definitively connected with the six classes;

<a id="u00454"></a>

they appear directly, together with their times.

<a id="u00455"></a>

The teachers of the past, future, and present,

<a id="u00456"></a>

the buddhas, the Blessed Ones,

<a id="u00457"></a>

with a single melody, in the maṇḍala,

<a id="u00458"></a>

for the appearance of the fortunate,

<a id="u00459"></a>

through the complete purity of the six faculties,

<a id="u00460"></a>

in tens apiece, from the three embodiments, [N-029](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-029)

<a id="u00461"></a>

proliferate in particular from the emanation embodiment.

<a id="u00462"></a>

Merely through a ray of that proliferation,

<a id="u00463"></a>

the yogin's stream of conceptual thought ceases.

<a id="u00464"></a>

The sites, distinctions, and domains of their vital cores [N-029](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-029)

<a id="u00465"></a>

are attained, and one assuredly enters seeing and realization.

<a id="u00466"></a>

Thus the purposes of sound

<a id="u00467"></a>

should be known in two aspects:

<a id="u00468"></a>

supreme and common—

<a id="u00469"></a>

the individually distinguishing magic for those with the appropriate karma.

<a id="u00470"></a>

For the supreme, the five embodiments are manifestly accomplished.

<a id="u00471"></a>

Through the ten signs of such accomplishment,

<a id="u00472"></a>

one equals the body of Vajradhara.

<a id="u00473"></a>

Its qualities cannot be exhausted in words.

<a id="u00474"></a>

In phenomena and the basic space of phenomena,

<a id="u00475"></a>

in the Ground, path, and result,

<a id="u00476"></a>

one plays in bliss.

<a id="u00477"></a>

The common purposes are these:

<a id="u00478"></a>

treatises on language and valid cognition,

<a id="u00479"></a>

crafts and various magical skills,

<a id="u00480"></a>

archery and activities not previously trained in are accomplished. [N-030](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-030)

<a id="u00481"></a>

One can act upon the five elements,

<a id="u00482"></a>

prevails over weapons and poison,

<a id="u00483"></a>

and becomes skilled in the sounds of the insentient and the aware,

<a id="u00484"></a>

in signs and conventions.

<a id="u00485"></a>

In brief, one comes to know the treatises on purposes

<a id="u00486"></a>

and the defining characteristics of the commands.

<a id="u00487"></a>

One distinguishes the varieties of medicines and elixir preparations,

<a id="u00488"></a>

brings ‘penetrating letters’—thal-yig—to perfection, [N-030](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-030)

<a id="u00489"></a>

and worldly applications are spontaneously accomplished.

<a id="u00490"></a>

Thus the purposes of word and meaning are accomplished.

<a id="u00491"></a>

The distinguishing magic definitive for the continuum is set apart.

<a id="u00492"></a>

**[Source heading: Fifth reply.]**

<a id="u00493"></a>

There are eight aspects of the sites of penetration:

<a id="u00494"></a>

world systems and buddha-fields,

<a id="u00495"></a>

faults in assertions and the sites of faults,

<a id="u00496"></a>

the activity of projection and application,

<a id="u00497"></a>

what is acknowledged and the crucial point of going—eight in all. [N-031](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-031) [N-T21](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t21)

<a id="u00498"></a>

These are held to be the sites of penetration.

<a id="u00499"></a>

Regarding a world system: the times of its formation,

<a id="u00500"></a>

abiding, and destruction;

<a id="u00501"></a>

emptiness, causes and conditions;

<a id="u00502"></a>

its manner of establishment and its measure of extension. [N-032](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-032)

<a id="u00503"></a>

The buddha-fields have thirteen aspects:

<a id="u00504"></a>

beneath the world here

<a id="u00505"></a>

is the realm called Melody of Penetration,

<a id="u00506"></a>

its extent difficult to measure, utterly beautiful.

<a id="u00507"></a>

Its causes, conditions, and intrinsic nature,

<a id="u00508"></a>

the teacher's teaching and its retinue,

<a id="u00509"></a>

above, below, and in the directions and intermediate directions—

<a id="u00510"></a>

its length and breadth are pleasing, well-arrayed and adorned.

<a id="u00511"></a>

Then, six worlds below,

<a id="u00512"></a>

that called Holder of Penetration

<a id="u00513"></a>

is wheel-shaped, with unimpeded penetration.

<a id="u00514"></a>

The teacher's teaching is truth itself;

<a id="u00515"></a>

well-adorned in its array, it is the second.

<a id="u00516"></a>

Three worlds to the right of this,

<a id="u00517"></a>

that called Protector of Penetration

<a id="u00518"></a>

is entirely adorned with emeralds.

<a id="u00519"></a>

Its light and colors are definitely clear;

<a id="u00520"></a>

it is a field where the teacher's teaching trains beings,

<a id="u00521"></a>

each with their own karma and characteristics.

<a id="u00522"></a>

Then, six worlds above,

<a id="u00523"></a>

that called Spreading Penetration

<a id="u00524"></a>

resembles the shape of an opened lotus,

<a id="u00525"></a>

very extensive and level.

<a id="u00526"></a>

Diverse light blazes; its colors

<a id="u00527"></a>

appear white and red, intermingling.

<a id="u00528"></a>

Coarse reflected forms do not appear.

<a id="u00529"></a>

The sources of the teacher's teaching expand;

<a id="u00530"></a>

with the age complete, it is called Penetration.

<a id="u00531"></a>

Above this world,

<a id="u00532"></a>

still higher, beyond nineteen worlds,

<a id="u00533"></a>

is that called Club of Penetration.

<a id="u00534"></a>

Its region is utterly complete and delightful. [N-033](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-033)[^G-U00534]

<a id="u00535"></a>

With diverse small jewels,

<a id="u00536"></a>

it is definitively adorned in the manner of heaps.

<a id="u00537"></a>

The teacher's teaching possesses the two completions;

<a id="u00538"></a>

it is an array of garlands of skulls.

<a id="u00539"></a>

In the world's northeastern intermediate direction,

<a id="u00540"></a>

that called Flow of Penetration

<a id="u00541"></a>

resembles a stūpa in shape, with beautiful tiers,

<a id="u00542"></a>

entirely adorned with five arrays. [N-033](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-033)[^G-U00542]

<a id="u00543"></a>

It is the Ground from which diverse sounds and melodies arise.

<a id="u00544"></a>

The teacher's teaching certainly arises at its time.

<a id="u00545"></a>

Twenty-one worlds above it,

<a id="u00546"></a>

is that called Fully Pervaded by Penetration.

<a id="u00547"></a>

In the intrinsic nature of a pure object of focus,

<a id="u00548"></a>

it appears with unimpeded penetration, without outside or inside.

<a id="u00549"></a>

The teacher's teaching displays arising;

<a id="u00550"></a>

its active causes and conditions: the All-Pervader. [N-032](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-032)

<a id="u00551"></a>

To the left, in this world's southeast,

<a id="u00552"></a>

beyond six world systems,

<a id="u00553"></a>

is that called Sound of Penetration,

<a id="u00554"></a>

thickly covered with moving pennants.

<a id="u00555"></a>

Victory banners, parasols, and so forth are raised.

<a id="u00556"></a>

With showers of scattered flowers and fragrance,

<a id="u00557"></a>

the teacher and the teaching

<a id="u00558"></a>

are surrounded by hosts of beautifying reflected forms.

<a id="u00559"></a>

The play of bliss arises.

<a id="u00560"></a>

To the right, in this world's southwest,

<a id="u00561"></a>

six worlds away and below, is Wind of Penetration.

<a id="u00562"></a>

Swaying and active in the open sky,

<a id="u00563"></a>

moving back and forth and floating,

<a id="u00564"></a>

it brings forth sounds through movement.

<a id="u00565"></a>

The teacher's teaching is stirred by wind.

<a id="u00566"></a>

There are three engagements endowed with five. [N-032](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-032)

<a id="u00567"></a>

Thirteen worlds stacked above it,

<a id="u00568"></a>

is that called Jewel Penetration,

<a id="u00569"></a>

adorned with auspicious suns,

<a id="u00570"></a>

ornamented with the qualities of desire,

<a id="u00571"></a>

beautiful with magical designs and letters.

<a id="u00572"></a>

The teacher's teaching is complete in reflected forms,

<a id="u00573"></a>

beautiful with ‘lda-ldi’ lotus eyes. [N-034](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-034)

<a id="u00574"></a>

Above this world's northwestern intermediate direction,

<a id="u00575"></a>

in the sixth world system,

<a id="u00576"></a>

is that called Gathering Penetration,

<a id="u00577"></a>

with goddesses whose offerings are complete,

<a id="u00578"></a>

filled with light and the fragrance of sandalwood.[^G-U00578]

<a id="u00579"></a>

The teacher's teaching is equal in its parts,

<a id="u00580"></a>

surrounded by beautiful forms, sixteen years of age,

<a id="u00581"></a>

utterly youthful.

<a id="u00582"></a>

Nine worlds below it,

<a id="u00583"></a>

is that called Conch-Endowed Deity of Penetration,

<a id="u00584"></a>

faithful and intoxicated by sweet fragrance,

<a id="u00585"></a>

with flashing, intensely blazing light,

<a id="u00586"></a>

densely filled with intensely tremulous brightness. [N-034](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-034)

<a id="u00587"></a>

The teacher and the teaching,

<a id="u00588"></a>

its retinue and enjoyments, are complete.

<a id="u00589"></a>

In the sixth world from here,

<a id="u00590"></a>

is that called Star Penetration.

<a id="u00591"></a>

Its form of stacked wheels

<a id="u00592"></a>

is completely piled up in fives and nines.

<a id="u00593"></a>

It is adorned with awnings and hanging garlands. [N-034](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-034)

<a id="u00594"></a>

Thunder roars and red tongues of lightning issue forth.[^G-U00594]

<a id="u00595"></a>

The teacher's teaching is surrounded by authoritative transmissions.

<a id="u00596"></a>

Garlands of arising light and the faces of nāgas [N-035](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-035)[^G-U00596]

<a id="u00597"></a>

illuminate all directions and intermediate directions.

<a id="u00598"></a>

**[Source heading: Sixth reply.]**

<a id="u00599"></a>

The phrases of one's own and others' assertions

<a id="u00600"></a>

are decisively resolved through one's own phrases. [N-036](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-036)

<a id="u00601"></a>

To bring one's own position to a definite conclusion,

<a id="u00602"></a>

through authoritative transmission, reasoning, and pith instructions,

<a id="u00603"></a>

remove faults, establish it, and abandon contradictions.

<a id="u00604"></a>

Abandon the three faults and dispel doubt.

<a id="u00605"></a>

Take up the four logical reasons and an assertion.

<a id="u00606"></a>

Seize the fortress of abiding and flee to the side. [N-036](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-036)

<a id="u00607"></a>

Draw a wheel in the center of the four directions.

<a id="u00608"></a>

Establish a logical reason free from taking sides.

<a id="u00609"></a>

Against others, debate the definitive application of a consequence. [N-T21](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t21)

<a id="u00610"></a>

Surround assertion, what is to be proved, and logical reason

<a id="u00611"></a>

with projections, three by three.

<a id="u00612"></a>

The skilled apply these with their defining characteristics complete.

<a id="u00613"></a>

This is the subject matter established for oneself and others.

<a id="u00614"></a>

Faults are of two kinds: one's own and others'.

<a id="u00615"></a>

Know one's own faults oneself.

<a id="u00616"></a>

In assertion, logical reason, and what is to be proved,

<a id="u00617"></a>

identify each individual fault

<a id="u00618"></a>

and bring the qualities to completion.

<a id="u00619"></a>

Know the other's qualities;

<a id="u00620"></a>

let the skilled examine that person's faults.

<a id="u00621"></a>

From the mere phrases expressing word and meaning,

<a id="u00622"></a>

through the defining characteristics of connection and nonconnection,

<a id="u00623"></a>

reckon the consequences for the pervaded meanings. [N-036](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-036)

<a id="u00624"></a>

**[Source heading: Seventh reply.]**

<a id="u00625"></a>

Projection is wind, the activity of transference.

<a id="u00626"></a>

It mounts phrases and angles. [N-037](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-037)[^G-U00626]

<a id="u00627"></a>

Above and below the wheel of logical reasons,

<a id="u00628"></a>

it penetrates the reverse and the angles.

<a id="u00629"></a>

Through the modes of subject and property, [N-T20](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t20)

<a id="u00630"></a>

engaging two by two, it projects.

<a id="u00631"></a>

Wind also projects the afflictions.

<a id="u00632"></a>

Through the impure [wind]—primordial knowing. [N-038](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-038)[^G-U00632]

<a id="u00633"></a>

With an object of focus for application, maintain one's own position.

<a id="u00634"></a>

Subject, assertion, and logical reason,

<a id="u00635"></a>

indicated by an example, form one application.

<a id="u00636"></a>

Know the subject's connection through the object.

<a id="u00637"></a>

Examine the faults of an assertion before making it.

<a id="u00638"></a>

Through logical reasons, reckon the meaning of the connection.

<a id="u00639"></a>

Through an example, establish the phenomena that appear.

<a id="u00640"></a>

Tenets depend on the conceptual mind,

<a id="u00641"></a>

through the expressiveness of knowing in oneself and others.

<a id="u00642"></a>

From what is acknowledged, consequences follow. [N-T21](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t21)

<a id="u00643"></a>

The activities of doing and of analysis,

<a id="u00644"></a>

differences between phrases and realization,

<a id="u00645"></a>

the various junctures at which causes and conditions ripen,

<a id="u00646"></a>

wishing to go and having gone,

<a id="u00647"></a>

with different particular modes of connection—

<a id="u00648"></a>

what is called ‘gone’: the objects,

<a id="u00649"></a>

paths and sites, phrases and engagement,

<a id="u00650"></a>

mindfulness, the actual, and what is not an entity, [N-039](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-039)

<a id="u00651"></a>

abiding, arising, and acting, [N-039](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-039)[^G-U00651]

<a id="u00652"></a>

paths with reversal and paths without reversal,

<a id="u00653"></a>

what has been accomplished and the act of accomplishing—

<a id="u00654"></a>

these are the causes of what is done and what does it.

<a id="u00655"></a>

**[Source heading: Eighth reply.]**

<a id="u00656"></a>

To draw the definitive reasons together at their conclusion:

<a id="u00657"></a>

transformation occurs through the magical display of the elements.

<a id="u00658"></a>

Because seasons change through time,

<a id="u00659"></a>

the yogin's experience and knowing also change.

<a id="u00660"></a>

Let the skilled discern the times of change.

<a id="u00661"></a>

Through the course of wind, fire, earth, and water,

<a id="u00662"></a>

fire, wind, earth, and water revolve in ‘four, two.’ [N-040](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-040)

<a id="u00663"></a>

Earth, wind, water, and fire—two agents of change.

<a id="u00664"></a>

Wind, earth, fire, and water, from five parts,

<a id="u00665"></a>

transform and act as the body's Ground.

<a id="u00666"></a>

Sites of emptiness—there are eight.

<a id="u00667"></a>

The expressiveness of knowing that brings transformation:

<a id="u00668"></a>

proliferation, coiling, and spreading,

<a id="u00669"></a>

gathering and signs—regarding these,

<a id="u00670"></a>

drawing in, abiding, and transforming,

<a id="u00671"></a>

wind, wind, earth, earth, fire, fire, water—

<a id="u00672"></a>

from each one's sixteen functions,

<a id="u00673"></a>

pervading, entering, causing abiding, and guarding.

<a id="u00674"></a>

‘Transmission-pairing,’ single-sided pairing, and one common grouping [N-040](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-040)

<a id="u00675"></a>

are the agents of change that make one heroic.

<a id="u00676"></a>

**[Source heading: Ninth reply.]**

<a id="u00677"></a>

Clearly distinguishing the two—chapters and their expression— [N-041](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-041)

<a id="u00678"></a>

the Array of Diversity, the abode of pith instructions,

<a id="u00679"></a>

is the first: definite arising.

<a id="u00680"></a>

It is the Ground, definitely showing what is yet to come.

<a id="u00681"></a>

Taking up the Ground together with the introductory setting,

<a id="u00682"></a>

the Array of the Gathered Crucial Points, the support of ordinary mind,

<a id="u00683"></a>

is the second: bringing [it] down upon itself. [N-041](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-041)

<a id="u00684"></a>

It shows what certainly arises from the secret command and the extent of change.

<a id="u00685"></a>

The third, the Array of Primordial Knowing,

<a id="u00686"></a>

shows the foundation for indication by words and signs.

<a id="u00687"></a>

The fourth, the Array of the Nature of Phenomena,

<a id="u00688"></a>

is the transmission on the nature of ordinary mind, complete in qualities. [N-T22](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t22)

<a id="u00689"></a>

The fifth is the Array of View and Cultivation,

<a id="u00690"></a>

the crucial point of basic space, complete in freedom from doing.

<a id="u00691"></a>

The sixth is the Array of Embodiments,

<a id="u00692"></a>

the secret Ground, complete in qualities.

<a id="u00693"></a>

Thus, through these six,

<a id="u00694"></a>

the result of the three embodiments is attained.

<a id="u00695"></a>

Know the chapter divisions that show their scope.

<a id="u00696"></a>

The treasure from which glorious enlightened intent arises,

<a id="u00697"></a>

also the embodiment of the method of bliss,

<a id="u00698"></a>

prophecy, guidance, and the crucial point of ordinary mind,

<a id="u00699"></a>

protecting beings and oneself, and the wind deity—

<a id="u00700"></a>

the remainder is just that much. [N-041](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-041)

<a id="u00701"></a>

**[Source heading: Tenth reply.]**

<a id="u00702"></a>

Regarding the explanation of ‘perfection’:

<a id="u00703"></a>

phrases of the teaching in which clinging is self-liberated,

<a id="u00704"></a>

a text with neither additions nor omissions,

<a id="u00705"></a>

and completeness without delusion in the meaning.

<a id="u00706"></a>

The explanation is free from conventions and phrases.

<a id="u00707"></a>

From the complete aspects of class-formation, [N-042](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-042)

<a id="u00708"></a>

the languages of the six classes of beings

<a id="u00709"></a>

and all engagements through signs appear.

<a id="u00710"></a>

Whatever is transformed by each one's capacity,[^G-U00710]

<a id="u00711"></a>

is complete in intrinsic nature; thus it is the Great Perfection.

<a id="u00712"></a>

The perfection distinguishing appearance and emptiness:

<a id="u00713"></a>

having clearly distinguished the text's phrases and meanings,

<a id="u00714"></a>

it is entirely complete, with nothing remaining.

<a id="u00715"></a>

The Ground itself is complete without change.

<a id="u00716"></a>

The path is straight and fully complete.

<a id="u00717"></a>

The result is complete in the condition in which nothing is to be done.

<a id="u00718"></a>

View and cultivation are complete in great freedom from doing.

<a id="u00719"></a>

Empowerment, pledges, and vows are complete.

<a id="u00720"></a>

In brief, everything is complete.

<a id="u00721"></a>

**[Source heading: Eleventh reply.]**

<a id="u00722"></a>

True phrases in the introductory setting:

<a id="u00723"></a>

for every individual being trained,

<a id="u00724"></a>

the Ground of training is the five aggregates.

<a id="u00725"></a>

The introductory setting is what trains that Ground.

<a id="u00726"></a>

‘Introducing’ refers to expression in phrases.

<a id="u00727"></a>

‘Ground’ has five aspects.

<a id="u00728"></a>

The place is held to have three aspects.

<a id="u00729"></a>

‘Phun’ means gathered together; [N-043](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-043)

<a id="u00730"></a>

‘Gsum’ is the number [three], that which brings appearance;

<a id="u00731"></a>

‘Tshogs’ is the completeness of qualities.

<a id="u00732"></a>

The teacher too has three aspects:

<a id="u00733"></a>

‘Phun’ means complete as embodiment;

<a id="u00734"></a>

‘Gsum’ makes the three embodiments appear;

<a id="u00735"></a>

‘Tshogs’ gathers the retinues.

<a id="u00736"></a>

The retinue too has three aspects:

<a id="u00737"></a>

‘Phun’ means arrayed as forms;

<a id="u00738"></a>

‘Gsum’ is the number [three], with diversity gathered;

<a id="u00739"></a>

‘Tshogs’ means having faith and gathering.

<a id="u00740"></a>

The teaching too has three aspects:

<a id="u00741"></a>

‘Phun’ means the quintessence of the innermost core;

<a id="u00742"></a>

‘Gsum’ is the precious collection of scriptures;

<a id="u00743"></a>

‘Tshogs’ liberates individuals.

<a id="u00744"></a>

Time too is three, in which the four are gathered:

<a id="u00745"></a>

‘Phun’ means all vehicles are complete;

<a id="u00746"></a>

‘Gsum’ is past, present, and future;

<a id="u00747"></a>

‘Tshogs’ is definite completeness.

<a id="u00748"></a>

Being uncommon, it is exceptional:

<a id="u00749"></a>

the sign of surpassing all vehicles,

<a id="u00750"></a>

the quintessences gathered into the core,

<a id="u00751"></a>

revealed by the teacher's speech to the retinue,

<a id="u00752"></a>

the foremost teaching, gathering the supremely wondrous.

<a id="u00753"></a>

As for the causes and conditions of the common introductory setting,

<a id="u00754"></a>

because it accords with others merely in the form of transmission,

<a id="u00755"></a>

it is known as ‘common.’

<a id="u00756"></a>

**[Source heading: Twelfth reply.]**

<a id="u00757"></a>

Thus, regarding this very teaching,

<a id="u00758"></a>

the remaining teaching is as follows:

<a id="u00759"></a>

the definite divisions of time are explained—

<a id="u00760"></a>

Endowed with the Core, Endowed with Perfection,

<a id="u00761"></a>

Great Endowment with Perfection,

<a id="u00762"></a>

likewise Endowed with Three and Endowed with Two,

<a id="u00763"></a>

Endowed with Conflict and Great Endowment with Conflict. [N-044](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-044)

<a id="u00764"></a>

Between each of these,

<a id="u00765"></a>

in sixty ages of darkness,

<a id="u00766"></a>

the shoots of the teaching will shift elsewhere.

<a id="u00767"></a>

In the north, at Endowed with Movement,

<a id="u00768"></a>

the lamp of the teaching will shine clearly.

<a id="u00769"></a>

A monk called Glorious Protector

<a id="u00770"></a>

will uphold it.

<a id="u00771"></a>

His patron, a king

<a id="u00772"></a>

called Jinamitra,

<a id="u00773"></a>

will provide the basis for restoring the teaching.

<a id="u00774"></a>

It will remain for a thousand and three hundred years.

<a id="u00775"></a>

Then an age of darkness

<a id="u00776"></a>

will remain for sixty [years]. [N-044](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-044)

<a id="u00777"></a>

Then, in the south, at Protector of the Grove,

<a id="u00778"></a>

on an island spacious and beautiful,

<a id="u00779"></a>

the life-pillar of the teaching will descend.

<a id="u00780"></a>

One called Glory of Joy,

<a id="u00781"></a>

of the class of those with long locks,

<a id="u00782"></a>

will uphold this teaching.

<a id="u00783"></a>

His patron, of the householder class,

<a id="u00784"></a>

called Accomplishment of Virtue,

<a id="u00785"></a>

will make this very sacred teaching

<a id="u00786"></a>

remain for a hundred years.

<a id="u00787"></a>

Then the age of darkness

<a id="u00788"></a>

will last a hundred and twenty years.

<a id="u00789"></a>

Then, in the east, at Wind of Bliss,

<a id="u00790"></a>

on the peak of Vajra-Arising Mountain,

<a id="u00791"></a>

this sacred teaching will descend.

<a id="u00792"></a>

A king called Sūtrade

<a id="u00793"></a>

will uphold the teaching.

<a id="u00794"></a>

His patron, a brahmin

<a id="u00795"></a>

called Jaya Ākar,

<a id="u00796"></a>

will sustain it for a thousand and four hundred years. [N-045](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-045)

<a id="u00797"></a>

After that, the age of darkness

<a id="u00798"></a>

will last five thousand years.

<a id="u00799"></a>

Then, in the west, on the Island of Loving Care,

<a id="u00800"></a>

at a place called Bharabhati Sāli, [N-045](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-045)[^G-U00800]

<a id="u00801"></a>

among the class of fortunate beings,

<a id="u00802"></a>

the three—innermost, quintessence, and core—will descend. [N-045](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-045)

<a id="u00803"></a>

The monk Protector of Bliss

<a id="u00804"></a>

will uphold this sacred teaching.

<a id="u00805"></a>

His patron, Holder of Heroes,

<a id="u00806"></a>

of the outcaste class, will for a thousand years

<a id="u00807"></a>

make this remain and increase.

<a id="u00808"></a>

After that, darkness itself

<a id="u00809"></a>

will remain for five thousand years.

<a id="u00810"></a>

Then it will descend here again,

<a id="u00811"></a>

at the place known as the Vajra Seat.

<a id="u00812"></a>

It will increase and blaze with light

<a id="u00813"></a>

for five hundred and fifty years.

<a id="u00814"></a>

Excellent Intelligence will uphold it.

<a id="u00815"></a>

His patron will be a ḍākinī

<a id="u00816"></a>

called Pure Cool-Maker.

<a id="u00817"></a>

After that, in this world,

<a id="u00818"></a>

earth, water, fire, and wind will clash together.

<a id="u00819"></a>

It will not remain, but become a great emptiness.

<a id="u00820"></a>

What is called an age, from its beginning,

<a id="u00821"></a>

will finally reach the time of destruction.

<a id="u00822"></a>

**[Source heading: Thirteenth reply.]**

<a id="u00823"></a>

The immediate cause of the teaching's destruction

<a id="u00824"></a>

in each of those places

<a id="u00825"></a>

will be an individual called one of perverse craving.

<a id="u00826"></a>

Each such individual will be

<a id="u00827"></a>

the immediate cause of destroying the entire teaching.

<a id="u00828"></a>

In the first time of darkness here,

<a id="u00829"></a>

in the west of Jambudvīpa,

<a id="u00830"></a>

a king will have six fingers on his hand,

<a id="u00831"></a>

with an extra blue tooth at the front.

<a id="u00832"></a>

On the elbow of his right arm

<a id="u00833"></a>

there will be a black mole in the shape of a scorpion,

<a id="u00834"></a>

extremely terrifying.

<a id="u00835"></a>

Through him, the buddha's teaching will become dark.

<a id="u00836"></a>

This will last sixty years.

<a id="u00837"></a>

After that, the first Glory-Holder will uphold it.

<a id="u00838"></a>

After a thousand and three hundred years,

<a id="u00839"></a>

again, in the north of Jambudvīpa,

<a id="u00840"></a>

on the summit of Majestic Mountain,

<a id="u00841"></a>

one in the form of a monk, lacking a loving mind,

<a id="u00842"></a>

will have two ears on his right side

<a id="u00843"></a>

and triangular eyes covered from below upward. [N-046](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-046)

<a id="u00844"></a>

He and a group of non-Buddhist proponents will dispute.

<a id="u00845"></a>

For sixty years there will be darkness.

<a id="u00846"></a>

After that, one called Deity of Bliss

<a id="u00847"></a>

will make it increase for a hundred years.

<a id="u00848"></a>

After that, in the northeast of Jambudvīpa,

<a id="u00849"></a>

beside the flowing Unfordable River,

<a id="u00850"></a>

one with a king's body, endowed with merit,

<a id="u00851"></a>

will have only one eye,

<a id="u00852"></a>

coarse limbs and bodily strength,

<a id="u00853"></a>

and be adorned with archery and magical skills.

<a id="u00854"></a>

He will suppress those who practice the teaching.

<a id="u00855"></a>

For sixty years, the teaching itself

<a id="u00856"></a>

will set here and move elsewhere.

<a id="u00857"></a>

After that, one in the form of a solitary buddha

<a id="u00858"></a>

will appear west of the Vajra Seat. [N-046](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-046)

<a id="u00859"></a>

He will make the sacred teaching itself

<a id="u00860"></a>

remain for a hundred and twenty years.

<a id="u00861"></a>

After that, in central Jambudvīpa,

<a id="u00862"></a>

at a place called the Golden City,

<a id="u00863"></a>

one with an elder's body will have a two-tipped tongue,

<a id="u00864"></a>

a small frame and short limbs.

<a id="u00865"></a>

He will quarrel with groups and take up weapons.

<a id="u00866"></a>

With his appearance, darkness will again

<a id="u00867"></a>

last sixty years.

<a id="u00868"></a>

After that, in eastern Jambudvīpa,

<a id="u00869"></a>

on the river called Lokita,

<a id="u00870"></a>

on its right bank,

<a id="u00871"></a>

one called Glorious Lion

<a id="u00872"></a>

will sustain it for a thousand and four hundred years.

<a id="u00873"></a>

Then again, in central Jambudvīpa,

<a id="u00874"></a>

on the bank of the great river Gaṅgā,

<a id="u00875"></a>

a king's son, on his arm,

<a id="u00876"></a>

will have something emerald-colored,

<a id="u00877"></a>

projecting prominently to a measure of four finger-widths. [N-046](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-046)

<a id="u00878"></a>

With his appearance, darkness will again

<a id="u00879"></a>

last sixty years.

<a id="u00880"></a>

After that, at the temple called

<a id="u00881"></a>

Kamalaśīla,

<a id="u00882"></a>

its master, Protector of Youth,

<a id="u00883"></a>

will sustain it for a thousand years.

<a id="u00884"></a>

After that, in southern Jambudvīpa,

<a id="u00885"></a>

in the town called Brahmā's Island,

<a id="u00886"></a>

one of the commoner class will have a lion's head.

<a id="u00887"></a>

Three months after his birth,

<a id="u00888"></a>

darkness will last for sixty [years]. [N-044](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-044)

<a id="u00889"></a>

After that, on Vulture Peak,

<a id="u00890"></a>

the monk Beautifying Youth

<a id="u00891"></a>

will sustain it for five hundred and fifty years.

<a id="u00892"></a>

After that, on the borders of Jambudvīpa,

<a id="u00893"></a>

in the land called Kashmir,

<a id="u00894"></a>

in the City Endowed with Sound,

<a id="u00895"></a>

one with a bird's body and a human face

<a id="u00896"></a>

will be born among those of low lineage.

<a id="u00897"></a>

Ten years after his birth,

<a id="u00898"></a>

darkness will last for sixty [years].

<a id="u00899"></a>

After that, in Jambudvīpa,

<a id="u00900"></a>

five yojanas east of its center,

<a id="u00901"></a>

through the monk Śākya Dzīka,

<a id="u00902"></a>

for ten years from then,

<a id="u00903"></a>

it will be like a blazing lamp. [N-045](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-045)

<a id="u00904"></a>

Thus, through seven successive stages,

<a id="u00905"></a>

it will increase and diminish in Jambudvīpa.

<a id="u00906"></a>

Then it will definitely rise

<a id="u00907"></a>

into the field of Beautiful Array.

<a id="u00908"></a>

When lifespans fall below ten years,

<a id="u00909"></a>

it will not remain here, but appear there.

<a id="u00910"></a>

**[Source heading: Fourteenth reply.]**[^G-U00910]

<a id="u00911"></a>

When its flourishing and decline are thus complete,

<a id="u00912"></a>

this core of the sacred teaching

<a id="u00913"></a>

will be in the world of Spacious Protection.

<a id="u00914"></a>

To make the buddha's teaching remain,

<a id="u00915"></a>

the teacher called Beautiful Utpala Flower,

<a id="u00916"></a>

the buddha, the Blessed One,

<a id="u00917"></a>

will be in the palace of Stacked Bliss-Making,

<a id="u00918"></a>

with a retinue of five thousand.

<a id="u00919"></a>

All will be great bodhisattvas,

<a id="u00920"></a>

having attained acceptance of non-arising phenomena,

<a id="u00921"></a>

and only those with virtuous minds will appear.

<a id="u00922"></a>

With the coming of that very teacher,

<a id="u00923"></a>

those of perverse craving will be definitively tamed.

<a id="u00924"></a>

This very sacred teaching,

<a id="u00925"></a>

for five thousand thousands of years,

<a id="u00926"></a>

will be made to remain as the core of the teaching. [N-047](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-047)

<a id="u00927"></a>

Then again, as before,

<a id="u00928"></a>

it will increase and diminish through seven successive stages.

<a id="u00929"></a>

After that, in the spacious world system

<a id="u00930"></a>

of Melodious Crest,

<a id="u00931"></a>

the teacher will be Infinite Destroyer of Samsara.

<a id="u00932"></a>

His retinue will be seven and a half hundreds,

<a id="u00933"></a>

all arhats whose contaminations are exhausted,

<a id="u00934"></a>

who have done what was to be done and laid down the burden.

<a id="u00935"></a>

He too will sustain the core of the teaching itself

<a id="u00936"></a>

for seven hundred thousand years.

<a id="u00937"></a>

Following the course of the earlier sequence,

<a id="u00938"></a>

after seven such periods of abiding,

<a id="u00939"></a>

for eighty-five ages,

<a id="u00940"></a>

the teachings will dissolve into the buddha's embodiment,

<a id="u00941"></a>

and in no world system whatever

<a id="u00942"></a>

will the buddha's teaching remain.

<a id="u00943"></a>

Then, in the lower world,

<a id="u00944"></a>

for ‘a million, six hundred thousand’ ages, [N-047](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-047)

<a id="u00945"></a>

the buddha's teaching will remain for a long time.

<a id="u00946"></a>

There will be no increase or decline in it.

<a id="u00947"></a>

It will bring beings to maturity.

<a id="u00948"></a>

**[Source heading: Fifteenth reply.]**[^G-U00948]

<a id="u00949"></a>

The teaching of a perfectly awakened buddha

<a id="u00950"></a>

is held to have two aspects:

<a id="u00951"></a>

the actual teaching and its reflected form.

<a id="u00952"></a>

Its reflected form has no fixed pattern:

<a id="u00953"></a>

this and that will appear in a mixed way. [N-048](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-048)

<a id="u00954"></a>

The sun makes [it] fully appear.

<a id="u00955"></a>

The three actual presences of embodiment, speech, and awakened mind—

<a id="u00956"></a>

whoever receives them will, without effort,

<a id="u00957"></a>

unfailingly attain buddhahood.

<a id="u00958"></a>

Therefore, the crucial point of blessing:

<a id="u00959"></a>

in the emanation of speech as well,

<a id="u00960"></a>

having carefully painted the teacher's colors,

<a id="u00961"></a>

fasten it beneath the left armpit.

<a id="u00962"></a>

If one reads and retains it at the proper times,

<a id="u00963"></a>

one will certainly, unfailingly awaken. [N-048](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-048)

<a id="u00964"></a>

Whoever constantly reads this continuum

<a id="u00965"></a>

will become as described above.

<a id="u00966"></a>

The seventeen definitive secret commands,

<a id="u00967"></a>

together with the nails of the teaching and the magical letters,

<a id="u00968"></a>

in the earliest field of the emanation embodiment,

<a id="u00969"></a>

were spoken by the teacher Samantabhadra

<a id="u00970"></a>

at the place of Dense Array.

<a id="u00971"></a>

Then, in the abode of Those with Long Locks,

<a id="u00972"></a>

the All-Seeing Vairocana

<a id="u00973"></a>

held this, bringing the five families to maturity.

<a id="u00974"></a>

After that, through my holding it,

<a id="u00975"></a>

as the sacred life-pillar of the teaching,

<a id="u00976"></a>

a thousand and two buddhas

<a id="u00977"></a>

will definitely arise in reliance on this.

<a id="u00978"></a>

Sentient beings born miraculously or from eggs,

<a id="u00979"></a>

from warmth or from wombs,

<a id="u00980"></a>

will be brought to maturity.

<a id="u00981"></a>

From this very teaching that trains beings,

<a id="u00982"></a>

twelve wheels of teaching will arise.

<a id="u00983"></a>

Its thirty-six deeds,

<a id="u00984"></a>

in dependence on embodiment, speech, and awakened mind,

<a id="u00985"></a>

will appear to the sentient beings who are to be trained.

<a id="u00986"></a>

After I have passed beyond sorrow,

<a id="u00987"></a>

in the western land of Uḍḍiyāna,

<a id="u00988"></a>

to the divine lady of Dhanakośa,

<a id="u00989"></a>

a fatherless son, Vajrahe, [N-049](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-049)

<a id="u00990"></a>

will uphold the sacred teaching.

<a id="u00991"></a>

Under his guidance, in the west,

<a id="u00992"></a>

a fortunate brahmin boy,

<a id="u00993"></a>

called Mañjuśrīpati,

<a id="u00994"></a>

will uphold it for seventy-five years.

<a id="u00995"></a>

After him, a fortunate one,[^G-U00995]

<a id="u00996"></a>

in the place called Sośa Island,

<a id="u00997"></a>

of the householder class, called Śrīsiddha,

<a id="u00998"></a>

will uphold it.

<a id="u00999"></a>

After him, one of the outcaste class will uphold it—

<a id="u01000"></a>

Jñānasūtra will uphold this very teaching. [N-049](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-049)

<a id="u01001"></a>

After him, one of the householder class—

<a id="u01002"></a>

Vast Seal—will uphold it. [N-050](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-050)

<a id="u01003"></a>

After him, one of the royal class,

<a id="u01004"></a>

an emanation together with an attendant, will uphold it. [N-050](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-050)

<a id="u01005"></a>

After him, in the form of an ascetic,

<a id="u01006"></a>

Spontaneous Victory Banner will do so.

<a id="u01007"></a>

After him, Sedheśvara will uphold it.

<a id="u01008"></a>

After him, those abiding on the grounds—

<a id="u01009"></a>

bodhisattvas—will uphold it.

<a id="u01010"></a>

After that, an emanation of Vajrapāṇi,

<a id="u01011"></a>

Vajraphala, will uphold it.

<a id="u01012"></a>

After that, the king Lion—

<a id="u01013"></a>

the Good One—will uphold it. [N-050](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-050)

<a id="u01014"></a>

After that, Glorious Word-Specialist will uphold it.

<a id="u01015"></a>

After that, Yogi Prabha will uphold it.

<a id="u01016"></a>

After that, King Throne-Light will do so.

<a id="u01017"></a>

After that, the monk Endowed with Glory;

<a id="u01018"></a>

then Supreme Intelligence will uphold it.

<a id="u01019"></a>

Then the ḍākinī Glory-Holder,

<a id="u01020"></a>

having upheld this very teaching,

<a id="u01021"></a>

will spread it when the lifespan is ten years.

<a id="u01022"></a>

Thereafter, its essence is non-abiding. [N-050](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-050)

<a id="u01023"></a>

**[Source heading: Sixteenth reply.]**[^G-U01023]

<a id="u01024"></a>

This secret heart-sphere of the teaching: [N-051](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-051) [N-T24](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t24)

<a id="u01025"></a>

after the seven teachings have passed in this way,

<a id="u01026"></a>

and after its definite course through two fields,

<a id="u01027"></a>

when the seven have thus passed, [N-051](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-051)

<a id="u01028"></a>

on the summit of the Vajra Seat,

<a id="u01029"></a>

the speech-token of all the teachings—[N-051](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-051)

<a id="u01030"></a>

the letters of the Great Naturally Arisen—

<a id="u01031"></a>

will descend together with sound. [N-T03](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t03)

<a id="u01032"></a>

Ten million ages from now,

<a id="u01033"></a>

to the Blessed One Wheel-Garland of Lightning

<a id="u01034"></a>

it will appear. [N-051](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-051)

<a id="u01035"></a>

Then, after three hundred years—

<a id="u01036"></a>

after those three [hundreds] have thus passed—

<a id="u01037"></a>

the vajra of the teaching's awakened mind, too, [N-T04](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t04)

<a id="u01038"></a>

will appear to that teacher.

<a id="u01039"></a>

Then, after five hundred years,

<a id="u01040"></a>

the teaching's embodiment, too, will descend there.

<a id="u01041"></a>

When the actual teaching has descended in this way,

<a id="u01042"></a>

the embers of the teaching will blaze up again.

<a id="u01043"></a>

Also among that teacher's retinue,

<a id="u01044"></a>

one called Noble Brahmā-Light,

<a id="u01045"></a>

endowed with miraculous power, toward perfection

<a id="u01046"></a>

will have intense faith and aspiration;

<a id="u01047"></a>

he will definitely uphold [the teaching] and become manifest. [N-052](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-052)

<a id="u01048"></a>

Beginning with him, the teacher's retinue—

<a id="u01049"></a>

twenty thousand noble hearers—

<a id="u01050"></a>

through the discipline teaching for one age,

<a id="u01051"></a>

will be established in the result of an arhat.

<a id="u01052"></a>

Then there will be six ages of darkness.

<a id="u01053"></a>

Then two hundred thousand arhats

<a id="u01054"></a>

will gather in his retinue.

<a id="u01055"></a>

Through the sūtra teaching for eight ages,

<a id="u01056"></a>

he will establish [them] at the culmination of provisional and definitive activity. [N-052](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-052)

<a id="u01057"></a>

Having conferred empowerment endowed with miraculous power,

<a id="u01058"></a>

he will definitely impel [them onward], and the teaching will be complete.

<a id="u01059"></a>

Again he will show the manner of passing beyond sorrow.

<a id="u01060"></a>

Then there will be two ages of darkness.

<a id="u01061"></a>

Then, when the teacher is born,

<a id="u01062"></a>

a retinue of great bodhisattvas—

<a id="u01063"></a>

a hundred thousand—will gather.

<a id="u01064"></a>

Foremost among those gathered, Glorious Great Terror

<a id="u01065"></a>

will thoroughly collect the teacher's teaching. [N-052](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-052)

<a id="u01066"></a>

Through the higher teaching for eight ages,

<a id="u01067"></a>

all will attain the result in one lifetime. [N-053](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-053)[^G-U01067]

<a id="u01068"></a>

At that time samsara will be emptied from its depths:

<a id="u01069"></a>

sentient beings will attain buddhahood;

<a id="u01070"></a>

there can be no appearance called the six destinies.

<a id="u01071"></a>

For twenty thousand ages,

<a id="u01072"></a>

the flow of sentient beings' samsara having been cut off, [N-T17](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t17)

<a id="u01073"></a>

embodied beings will not appear at all.

<a id="u01074"></a>

Then, through particular karmic differences,

<a id="u01075"></a>

although sentient beings' afflictions are not manifest,

<a id="u01076"></a>

through the force of dormant tendencies rising up,

<a id="u01077"></a>

the forms taken on will be extremely subtle.

<a id="u01078"></a>

From these exceedingly subtle, numerous [forms],

<a id="u01079"></a>

the bodies of sentient beings endowed with form will develop. [N-052](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-052)

<a id="u01080"></a>

Thus, the inconceivable final extent of the teaching,

<a id="u01081"></a>

which cannot be encompassed by expression,

<a id="u01082"></a>

is for now indicated through words.

<a id="u01083"></a>

**[Source heading: Seventeenth reply.]**

<a id="u01084"></a>

As for the body and branches of the continuum:

<a id="u01085"></a>

the arrays in its body number two hundred

<a id="u01086"></a>

and exactly fifty. [N-054](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-054)

<a id="u01087"></a>

First, through clarity of ascertainment,

<a id="u01088"></a>

in the branches of the manifold array,

<a id="u01089"></a>

there are exactly seventy questions;

<a id="u01090"></a>

the arrays of the branches number one hundred and one. [N-054](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-054)[^G-U01090]

<a id="u01091"></a>

The body itself is thereby completed.

<a id="u01092"></a>

Its branches number two hundred and ten.

<a id="u01093"></a>

The foregoing body and branches are complete.

<a id="u01094"></a>

The body is complete; the branches disclose the secret. [N-054](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-054)[^G-U01094]

<a id="u01095"></a>

The second body is closely taught:

<a id="u01096"></a>

in the array gathering the crucial points,

<a id="u01097"></a>

from twenty-eight questions,

<a id="u01098"></a>

there are thirty-one arrays of ‘crucial-point times’ (gnad dus). [N-054](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-054)

<a id="u01099"></a>

Through these the body itself is complete.

<a id="u01100"></a>

The branches number two hundred and eight.

<a id="u01101"></a>

Through these, the second body

<a id="u01102"></a>

and its branches are complete.

<a id="u01103"></a>

The third body is closely taught:

<a id="u01104"></a>

for twenty-two questions,

<a id="u01105"></a>

there are fifty-eight arrays of primordial knowing;

<a id="u01106"></a>

the branches themselves number two hundred.

<a id="u01107"></a>

The branches of the third body are complete.

<a id="u01108"></a>

As for the fourth body and its branches:

<a id="u01109"></a>

there are twenty-one questions,

<a id="u01110"></a>

thirty-two arrays of the nature of phenomena,

<a id="u01111"></a>

and fifty-eight branches.

<a id="u01112"></a>

As for the fifth body and its branches:

<a id="u01113"></a>

there are eleven questions,

<a id="u01114"></a>

thirty-two arrays of viewing and cultivation,

<a id="u01115"></a>

and sixty-four branches.

<a id="u01116"></a>

The fifth body and its branches are complete.

<a id="u01117"></a>

As for the sixth body and its branches:

<a id="u01118"></a>

from the eight questions asked,

<a id="u01119"></a>

there are sixteen arrays of embodiments;

<a id="u01120"></a>

their branches number thirty-two.

<a id="u01121"></a>

The sixth body and its branches are complete.

<a id="u01122"></a>

**[Source heading: Eighteenth reply.]**

<a id="u01123"></a>

The account of the elements is as follows.

<a id="u01124"></a>

Through the particulars of shared karma,

<a id="u01125"></a>

sentient beings, with karma as their support,

<a id="u01126"></a>

depend on the outer elements.

<a id="u01127"></a>

Actions, clothing, and faculties, [N-055](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-055)

<a id="u01128"></a>

retinues and births, are held to become complete.

<a id="u01129"></a>

The sequence of activity of the inner elements is this:

<a id="u01130"></a>

earth makes the body's foundation; [N-055](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-055)

<a id="u01131"></a>

through its generation, maturation into flesh is held to occur.

<a id="u01132"></a>

Water gathers the whole body together;

<a id="u01133"></a>

through its generation, maturation into blood is held to occur.

<a id="u01134"></a>

Fire brings the whole body to maturity;

<a id="u01135"></a>

through its generation, maturation into warmth is held to occur.
