<a id="u01136"></a>

Wind raises the whole body;

<a id="u01137"></a>

through its generation, it brings about maturation into breath.

<a id="u01138"></a>

Through the particulars of their word and meaning,

<a id="u01139"></a>

the general aspects are definitely taught. [N-055](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-055)

<a id="u01140"></a>

Through training with form, the body will be exhausted. [N-056](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-056)

<a id="u01141"></a>

Through sound, languages will be known. [N-T03](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t03)

<a id="u01142"></a>

Through smell, taking nourishment from vital essences is accomplished. [N-T16](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t16)

<a id="u01143"></a>

Through taste, substances are transformed into nectar.

<a id="u01144"></a>

*Source annotation moved to endnotes; no root verse at U01144.*[^G-U01144]

<a id="u01145"></a>

The nectar completes the benefit of other sentient beings

<a id="u01146"></a>

and also one's own benefit. [N-056](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-056)

<a id="u01147"></a>

**[Source heading: Nineteenth reply.]**[^G-U01147]

<a id="u01148"></a>

The times of sentient beings have eight aspects:

<a id="u01149"></a>

illness, wind, and heat;

<a id="u01150"></a>

knowing, afflictions, awakening ordinary mind, [N-T25](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t25)

<a id="u01151"></a>

primordial knowing itself, and the elements. [N-057](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-057)

<a id="u01152"></a>

In the four times—summer, winter, autumn, and spring—

<a id="u01153"></a>

wind, bile, phlegm, and their conjunction, the four,

<a id="u01154"></a>

shift from their respective places.

<a id="u01155"></a>

This is the time of sentient beings' wind-mind shifting.

<a id="u01156"></a>

At this time wind is layered,

<a id="u01157"></a>

or slow and interrupted,

<a id="u01158"></a>

or subtle and hot. [N-057](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-057)

<a id="u01159"></a>

This shortens lifespans;

<a id="u01160"></a>

it steals knowing and brings stupefaction.

<a id="u01161"></a>

At intervals [knowing] becomes distracted and shifts.

<a id="u01162"></a>

Through the gathering of the five afflictions,

<a id="u01163"></a>

[beings] are projected into places of lower destiny.

<a id="u01164"></a>

Awakening ordinary mind dissipates, [N-T25](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t25)

<a id="u01165"></a>

flutters greatly, and becomes subtle. [N-057](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-057)

<a id="u01166"></a>

Primordial knowing is not clear.

<a id="u01167"></a>

The elements proliferate from their dispersal and penetration. [N-057](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-057)

<a id="u01168"></a>

This is the time of an individual sentient being.

<a id="u01169"></a>

As for the times common to sentient beings:

<a id="u01170"></a>

from the first, immeasurable [lifespan],

<a id="u01171"></a>

to the final lifespan of ten years,

<a id="u01172"></a>

sixty successive periods occur.

<a id="u01173"></a>

Because afflictions change under the power of karma,

<a id="u01174"></a>

through the karma of concordant, common purposes, [N-057](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-057)

<a id="u01175"></a>

from a lifespan of a thousand years,

<a id="u01176"></a>

also among the people of Jambudvīpa,

<a id="u01177"></a>

down to a hundred, the successive periods

<a id="u01178"></a>

number thirty.

<a id="u01179"></a>

Beginning from there, down to ten,

<a id="u01180"></a>

thirty successive periods occur.

<a id="u01181"></a>

Through eight stages in the times when habituation shifts,

<a id="u01182"></a>

activity subsequently becomes worse.

<a id="u01183"></a>

Periods of afflicting spirits and famine,

<a id="u01184"></a>

weapons and illness, arise.

<a id="u01185"></a>

Their particulars are many: these are the successive periods. [N-057](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-057)

<a id="u01186"></a>

**[Source heading: Twentieth reply.]**[^G-U01186]

<a id="u01187"></a>

Among the outer elements, wind

<a id="u01188"></a>

is joined with the element of space.

<a id="u01189"></a>

Upon it is water, and above that, earth.

<a id="u01190"></a>

The earth's extent and thickness are equal. [N-058](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-058)

<a id="u01191"></a>

‘Four innumerables and one million’;

<a id="u01192"></a>

‘one is a thousand of its quarter.’ [Numerical construction unresolved.] [N-058](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-058)

<a id="u01193"></a>

There are the arrays of continents and Mount Meru,

<a id="u01194"></a>

together with wish-granting trees and jewels,

<a id="u01195"></a>

the sun and moon, and the respective gods,

<a id="u01196"></a>

surrounded by the depths of the great ocean.

<a id="u01197"></a>

The Meru mountains and tributary streams

<a id="u01198"></a>

are definitely arrayed with seven precious substances.

<a id="u01199"></a>

Inwardly, first there is the formless [realm];

<a id="u01200"></a>

then the form realm, clear light;

<a id="u01201"></a>

gradually, also in the desire realm,

<a id="u01202"></a>

from the stainless moon

<a id="u01203"></a>

and the sun that makes things shine brightly,

<a id="u01204"></a>

the world system proliferates. [N-058](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-058)

<a id="u01205"></a>

Beginning with Mastery over Others' Emanations, in descending order,

<a id="u01206"></a>

then the abodes of the demigods,

<a id="u01207"></a>

then the humans of the four continents, successively.

<a id="u01208"></a>

Through afflictions together with selfhood,

<a id="u01209"></a>

the places of lower destiny gradually form.

<a id="u01210"></a>

The respective beings of the six classes

<a id="u01211"></a>

form within; their bodily size

<a id="u01212"></a>

and lifespan, through shared karma,

<a id="u01213"></a>

and their respective abodes and merit,

<a id="u01214"></a>

abide in accordance with their continent. [N-058](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-058)

<a id="u01215"></a>

The meaning is destruction and formation,

<a id="u01216"></a>

abiding itself and becoming empty;

<a id="u01217"></a>

birth, aging, illness, and death—

<a id="u01218"></a>

the mechanism of existence itself.

<a id="u01219"></a>

**[Source heading: Twenty-first reply.]**

<a id="u01220"></a>

The shifting of times is as follows.

<a id="u01221"></a>

From the initial immeasurable [lifespan]

<a id="u01222"></a>

down to the final ten [years],

<a id="u01223"></a>

there are increases upward and decreases downward.

<a id="u01224"></a>

Illness, famine, and weapons—

<a id="u01225"></a>

the three minor ages—join the intervals.

<a id="u01226"></a>

When sixty have thus passed,

<a id="u01227"></a>

seven [destructions] are by fire, and seven by water.

<a id="u01228"></a>

When these fourteen have been completed,

<a id="u01229"></a>

the remaining [destruction] is by wind. [N-059](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-059)

<a id="u01230"></a>

Thus there are sixty [periods] of abiding,

<a id="u01231"></a>

and the duration of destruction is as long;

<a id="u01232"></a>

for sixty [periods] it abides empty.

<a id="u01233"></a>

Thus, from one hundred and eighty [periods]— [N-059](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-059)[^G-U01233]

<a id="u01234"></a>

the duration is reckoned as the first age.

<a id="u01235"></a>

Likewise, with one hundred and eighty,

<a id="u01236"></a>

there is the second, intermediate age.

<a id="u01237"></a>

Through that many such repetitions— [N-059](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-059)[^G-U01237][^G-U01233]

<a id="u01238"></a>

there is one great age.[^G-U01233]

<a id="u01239"></a>

**[Source heading: Twenty-second reply.]**[^G-U01239]

<a id="u01240"></a>

Three tens of days

<a id="u01241"></a>

constitute one month.

<a id="u01242"></a>

When twelve such [months] are complete,

<a id="u01243"></a>

they are reckoned as one year.

<a id="u01244"></a>

When seven hundred

<a id="u01245"></a>

and twenty mornings and evenings are complete,

<a id="u01246"></a>

they are reckoned as three hundred and sixty days.

<a id="u01247"></a>

Their completion constitutes one year.

<a id="u01248"></a>

Days are distinguished as male and female according to activity. [N-060](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-060)

<a id="u01249"></a>

The months of autumn, spring, summer, and winter—

<a id="u01250"></a>

three for each—

<a id="u01251"></a>

enter into operation and enact the five aspects of the elements.

<a id="u01252"></a>

Rat, ox, and tiger;

<a id="u01253"></a>

long-eared hare, snake, and dragon;

<a id="u01254"></a>

horse, sheep, and monkey;

<a id="u01255"></a>

bird, dog, and pig—through these twelve,

<a id="u01256"></a>

the elemental calculations are extended. [N-061](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-061)

<a id="u01257"></a>

**[Source heading: Twenty-third reply.]**[^G-U01257]

<a id="u01258"></a>

The rites for increasing and restoring life,

<a id="u01259"></a>

accomplishing it and making it flourish—

<a id="u01260"></a>

when a yogin knows how to apply them,

<a id="u01261"></a>

[the lifespan] becomes equal to the sun and moon. [N-062](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-062)

<a id="u01262"></a>

For others, from equalizing and dismantling,

<a id="u01263"></a>

there are summoning, binding, and bringing down a slayer,

<a id="u01264"></a>

expelling, and the activities of killing.

<a id="u01265"></a>

When a yogin knows how to summon, [N-062](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-062)[^G-U01265]

<a id="u01266"></a>

beings are gathered under his power.

<a id="u01267"></a>

Increasing and restoring ascendancy,

<a id="u01268"></a>

making it abide and become established,

<a id="u01269"></a>

and, further, the application of equalizing—

<a id="u01270"></a>

these a yogin should undertake. [N-062](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-062)[^G-U01270]

<a id="u01271"></a>

**[Source heading: Twenty-fourth reply.]**

<a id="u01272"></a>

Dispersal, penetration, and equalizing comprise twelve:

<a id="u01273"></a>

for earth, water, fire, and wind, the four,

<a id="u01274"></a>

three each make twelve. [N-063](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-063)

<a id="a2000-c01-s01"></a>

*Scan-restored main verses:*[^G-A2000-C01-S01]

Water's penetration gathers the faculties;<br />
its dispersal causes disturbance;<br />
its equalizing produces the result.

<a id="u01275"></a>

Earth's penetration produces particles;

<a id="u01276"></a>

its dispersal produces branches;

<a id="u01277"></a>

through its equalizing, the body's elements are complete.

<a id="u01278"></a>

Fire's penetration produces illness;

<a id="u01279"></a>

through its dispersal, the body will abide;

<a id="u01280"></a>

its equalizing makes the elements abide.

<a id="u01281"></a>

Wind's penetration protects the elements;

<a id="u01282"></a>

its dispersal destroys other activities;

<a id="u01283"></a>

its equalizing gathers wind-mind into one.

<a id="u01284"></a>

These are the limits of what the three do.

<a id="u01285"></a>

Through water's penetration, earth gathers together.

<a id="u01286"></a>

*Source annotation moved to endnotes; no root verse at U01286.*[^G-U01286]

<a id="u01287"></a>

When penetration meets penetration,

<a id="u01288"></a>

nothing is accomplished, and destruction follows. [N-063](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-063)

<a id="u01289"></a>

Through dispersal itself, illness occurs;

<a id="u01290"></a>

through equalizing, elixir preparations and medicines are accomplished.

<a id="u01291"></a>

Fire and wind together destroy the aggregates;

<a id="u01292"></a>

through dispersal, all activities are accomplished;

<a id="u01293"></a>

through equalizing, activities of conflict reach their culmination.

<a id="u01294"></a>

Through water and fire, purposes are thoroughly accomplished;

<a id="u01295"></a>

through dispersal, one enters another;

<a id="u01296"></a>

through equalizing, long life and ascendancy are accomplished.

<a id="u01297"></a>

Through earth and wind, pacification is accomplished;

<a id="u01298"></a>

through dispersal, increase and subjugation are enacted;

<a id="u01299"></a>

through equalizing, aggressive ritual activities are accomplished. [N-063](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-063)

<a id="u01300"></a>

As for the activity of wind and water:

<a id="u01301"></a>

through dispersal, all virtuous activities are accomplished;

<a id="u01302"></a>

through equalizing, there is victory in warfare.

<a id="u01303"></a>

Through earth and fire, increase is accomplished;

<a id="u01304"></a>

through dispersal, transferring the elements is accomplished;

<a id="u01305"></a>

through equalizing, the slayer's rite is accomplished.

<a id="u01306"></a>

At the culmination of the activities of penetration and dispersal,

<a id="u01307"></a>

[these results] will definitely occur, Deity of the Faculties. [N-063](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-063)

<a id="u01308"></a>

**[Source heading: Twenty-fifth reply.]**[^G-U01308]

<a id="u01309"></a>

To cut the karmic continuum of samsara:

<a id="u01310"></a>

complete in freedom from doing, it is complete.

<a id="u01311"></a>

Free from extremes, it does not abide in a center.

<a id="u01312"></a>

Entirely complete, it is free from entities.

<a id="u01313"></a>

Without remainder, it is not a portion.

<a id="u01314"></a>

Not being one, the two extremes are exhausted.

<a id="u01315"></a>

Not appearing as many, it is beyond one.

<a id="u01316"></a>

Inexpressible, [it is] the Ground of words. [N-064](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-064)

<a id="u01317"></a>

Free from entities and empty: the core meaning.

<a id="u01318"></a>

Not falling to any side: the meaning of the view.

<a id="u01319"></a>

Knowing this blocks the path of samsara.

<a id="u01320"></a>

Direct perception arising from the crucial point itself—

<a id="u01321"></a>

through the power of familiarity, delusion is destroyed.

<a id="u01322"></a>

With intrinsic nature pure, there is no distraction.

<a id="u01323"></a>

Abiding continuously, there is no separation.

<a id="u01324"></a>

Being self-appearance, differentiating conceptualization is exhausted.

<a id="u01325"></a>

Wind exhausted, one engages in primordial knowing itself.

<a id="u01326"></a>

Through never being separate from this,

<a id="u01327"></a>

one cuts the flow of the paths of the six destinies.

<a id="u01328"></a>

Without making or contriving,

<a id="u01329"></a>

through activity by its own momentum, delusion is purified. [N-064](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-064)

<a id="u01330"></a>

Arising in letting be, there is non-arising.

<a id="u01331"></a>

Without grasping or attachment, one plays as one pleases.

<a id="u01332"></a>

Settled into its own condition, turbidity clears.

<a id="u01333"></a>

Being intrinsic nature, it is beyond methods.

<a id="u01334"></a>

This, too, blocks the gate of samsara.

<a id="u01335"></a>

**[Source heading: Twenty-sixth reply.]**

<a id="u01336"></a>

The qualities of the teacher, the buddha, the Blessed One,

<a id="u01337"></a>

cannot be encompassed by expression.

<a id="u01338"></a>

Through sixty excellences,

<a id="u01339"></a>

he upholds the foundation of the sacred teaching. [N-065](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-065)

<a id="u01340"></a>

For a long time in the world system,

<a id="u01341"></a>

the buddha's teaching abides.

<a id="u01342"></a>

Its qualities cannot be encompassed by expression.

<a id="u01343"></a>

From what do these arise?

<a id="u01344"></a>

From embodiment, speech, and awakened mind, [N-T04](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t04)

<a id="u01345"></a>

and three sets of twelve deeds:

<a id="u01346"></a>

the teacher having come into the world,

<a id="u01347"></a>

these are the qualities for those to be trained. [N-065](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-065)

<a id="u01348"></a>

Through the outer, inner, and secret four activities,

<a id="u01349"></a>

there are immeasurable qualities that purify.

<a id="u01350"></a>

These follow the sequence taught before.

<a id="u01351"></a>

The teaching's growth and completion, too,

<a id="u01352"></a>

are qualities of the Blessed One.

<a id="u01353"></a>

**[Source heading: Twenty-seventh reply.]**[^G-U01353]

<a id="u01354"></a>

In the sequence of training in the three embodiments,

<a id="u01355"></a>

making the desired purposes of the elements primary,

<a id="u01356"></a>

above all, by training in the sounds

<a id="u01357"></a>

of earth, water, fire, and wind, one definitely accomplishes [them]. [N-066](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-066) [N-T03](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t03)

<a id="u01358"></a>

Water's sound, gushing,

<a id="u01359"></a>

holds the melody of the ḍākinīs.

<a id="u01360"></a>

Through constant familiarity with this,

<a id="u01361"></a>

the emanation embodiment, too, is definitely accomplished.

<a id="u01362"></a>

Earth's sound is cool and heavy,

<a id="u01363"></a>

endowed with the voice of Great Brahmā.

<a id="u01364"></a>

Through constant, stable engagement in this,

<a id="u01365"></a>

the complete enjoyment embodiment is definitely accomplished.

<a id="u01366"></a>

For fire's sound, train in lengtheners; [N-066](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-066)

<a id="u01367"></a>

it reveals the melodic speech of the Great All-Pervader.

<a id="u01368"></a>

Whoever engages in listening to this

<a id="u01369"></a>

will definitely attain the qualities of the dharma embodiment.

<a id="u01370"></a>

Wind's sound is pressing and fierce;

<a id="u01371"></a>

it expresses the application of the king of sky-soarers. [N-066](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-066)

<a id="u01372"></a>

Knowing how to repeat this constantly,

<a id="u01373"></a>

one should undertake the common training in the three embodiments.

<a id="u01374"></a>

Thus the word and meaning of the four elements

<a id="u01375"></a>

are definitely joined to the outer times:

<a id="u01376"></a>

winter, spring, summer, and autumn;

<a id="u01377"></a>

water, earth, fire, and wind.

<a id="u01378"></a>

Definitely relating their sequence to the times,

<a id="u01379"></a>

and bringing it into accord with the yogin's body,

<a id="u01380"></a>

there is no doubt that accomplishment occurs. [N-066](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-066)

<a id="u01381"></a>

**[Source heading: Twenty-eighth reply.]**

<a id="u01382"></a>

As for the culmination of common worldly activities:

<a id="u01383"></a>

one wishing to fly relies on a pill. [N-067](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-067)

<a id="u01384"></a>

One wishing to become invisible rests the ordinary mind;

<a id="u01385"></a>

into a net of empty spheres,

<a id="u01386"></a>

placing both body and ordinary mind, one accomplishes [invisibility].

<a id="u01387"></a>

One wishing to attain unimpeded penetration

<a id="u01388"></a>

holds wind and places it in a sphere.

<a id="u01389"></a>

At intervals, knowing, like mist,

<a id="u01390"></a>

is to be brought out from Brahmā's abode. [N-067](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-067)

<a id="u01391"></a>

One wishing to overcome weapons and poison

<a id="u01392"></a>

directs the ordinary mind toward the four elements.

<a id="u01393"></a>

When one combines [them] into powder and performs the activity,

<a id="u01394"></a>

these are definitely accomplished. [N-067](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-067)

<a id="u01395"></a>

To overcome dogs, bears, and wolves,

<a id="u01396"></a>

one voices the four elements.

<a id="u01397"></a>

When the four dependent connections are made,

<a id="u01398"></a>

one will overcome them.

<a id="u01399"></a>

One wishing to be freed from the eight fears

<a id="u01400"></a>

cultivates a wheel endowed with a design

<a id="u01401"></a>

at the crown; cultivating wind,

<a id="u01402"></a>

through this, too, one overcomes those [fears].

<a id="u01403"></a>

One wishing to accomplish eye medicine—

<a id="u01404"></a>

the preparation involving crow's wings—

<a id="u01405"></a>

combines [it] properly and constantly repeats

<a id="u01406"></a>

the letters of the clarifiers. [N-067](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-067)

<a id="u01407"></a>

Then even the three realms appear clearly.

<a id="u01408"></a>

In warfare, trade, and arranging a marriage,

<a id="u01409"></a>

whoever wishes to prevail

<a id="u01410"></a>

takes account of the time and direction of the elements.

<a id="u01411"></a>

When these are made free from confusion,

<a id="u01412"></a>

there is no doubt of definite accomplishment. [N-067](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-067)

<a id="u01413"></a>

Whoever wishes to become a sword-holder,

<a id="u01414"></a>

with a preparation of yellow sa-pa-la, [N-068](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-068)[^G-U01414]

<a id="u01415"></a>

intensely repeats the elemental letters.

<a id="u01416"></a>

When [these] are properly applied at the body's five places,[^G-U01416]

<a id="u01417"></a>

and the time of the elements is thoroughly absorbed, [N-068](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-068)[^G-U01417]

<a id="u01418"></a>

through teaching the thorough application,

<a id="u01419"></a>

one becomes a great sword-holder.

<a id="u01420"></a>

A person gathering ḍākinīs under his power,

<a id="u01421"></a>

having gone to a place where mamos assemble,

<a id="u01422"></a>

a preparation of precious substances— [N-068](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-068)[^G-U01422]

<a id="u01423"></a>

at the time when the heads of the elements meet, [N-068](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-068)

<a id="u01424"></a>

properly combines [them] into the pill itself. [N-068](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-068)

<a id="u01425"></a>

Whoever holds it inside the mouth

<a id="u01426"></a>

will bring the ḍākinīs under his power.

<a id="u01427"></a>

One wishing to accomplish swift feet

<a id="u01428"></a>

adorns sixteen vajra wings

<a id="u01429"></a>

with A and YAṂ.

<a id="u01430"></a>

At the time when the bird preparation arises,

<a id="u01431"></a>

without error concerning the time,

<a id="u01432"></a>

one smears paste on the soles of the feet

<a id="u01433"></a>

in accordance with the number. [N-068](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-068)

<a id="u01434"></a>

In an instant, there is no doubt

<a id="u01435"></a>

that one will travel throughout Jambudvīpa.

<a id="u01436"></a>

Also, to travel through charnel-ground places,

<a id="u01437"></a>

one properly combines the eye-preparation pill

<a id="u01438"></a>

at its own times of application,

<a id="u01439"></a>

and surrounds it with A. [N-068](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-068)[^G-U01439]

<a id="u01440"></a>

Taking the vitality of the elements of one's own year,

<a id="u01441"></a>

by going to the place, one definitely accomplishes [this]. [N-068](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-068)

<a id="u01442"></a>

One wishing to take nourishment from vital essences: [N-T16](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t16)

<a id="u01443"></a>

the method of combining the five nectars,

<a id="u01444"></a>

and, further, balancing the elements—

<a id="u01445"></a>

a skilled person properly combines them in equal parts

<a id="u01446"></a>

and pours [them] into a precious vessel.

<a id="u01447"></a>

Then honey and ud-tsid-ta

<a id="u01448"></a>

are properly added as supplements. [N-069](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-069)

<a id="u01449"></a>

Taking a drop of mercury at a time, [Editorial safety note: Do not carry out this prescription.] [N-069](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-069)

<a id="u01450"></a>

likewise, [a portion] the size of a bean—

<a id="u01451"></a>

if those skilled consume [it],

<a id="u01452"></a>

there is no doubt that it becomes

<a id="u01453"></a>

a great [practice of] taking nourishment from vital essences. [N-069](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-069)

<a id="u01454"></a>

**[Source heading: Twenty-ninth reply.]**

<a id="u01455"></a>

When accomplishing the particular embodiments,

<a id="u01456"></a>

one trains in the smells of the four elements.

<a id="u01457"></a>

Water's smell gives bliss.

<a id="u01458"></a>

Adding [that] whose consuming power greatly increases in this,

<a id="u01459"></a>

and joining [it] to wind, [N-070](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-070)

<a id="u01460"></a>

one accomplishes the embodiment of the vajra-holder.

<a id="u01461"></a>

Earth's smell gathers realization.

<a id="u01462"></a>

Through the manner of preparing palāśa

<a id="u01463"></a>

and continually teaching dependent connections,

<a id="u01464"></a>

the embodiment of essence itself, too, is accomplished. [N-070](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-070)

<a id="u01465"></a>

Fire's smell generates experience.

<a id="u01466"></a>

Through familiarity with the experience

<a id="u01467"></a>

of one's own body being clear, Heruka, too, is accomplished.

<a id="u01468"></a>

Wind's smell is an object of training.

<a id="u01469"></a>

Combining [it] through the activity of abiding and entering,

<a id="u01470"></a>

if one clearly cultivates the sequence of the definite body,

<a id="u01471"></a>

the embodiments of the five families are well accomplished. [N-070](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-070)

<a id="u01472"></a>

Furthermore, through training in taste and touch themselves,

<a id="u01473"></a>

according to their respective sequences,

<a id="u01474"></a>

there is no doubt that whichever embodiments

<a id="u01475"></a>

one desires are accomplished.

<a id="u01476"></a>

**[Source heading: Thirtieth reply.]**[^G-U01476]

<a id="u01477"></a>

Whoever cultivates the yoga of sleep

<a id="u01478"></a>

thereby makes dull confusion into the path. [N-071](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-071)

<a id="u01479"></a>

For the outer times, from the Ground, [N-071](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-071)

<a id="u01480"></a>

one trains the expressiveness of the yogic body.

<a id="u01481"></a>

The crucial point is to hold the channels at their sources.

<a id="u01482"></a>

Through the four inner times themselves,

<a id="u01483"></a>

one trains the expressiveness of yoga.

<a id="u01484"></a>

The crucial point is to cut the flows of expression.

<a id="u01485"></a>

Through the four secret times, too,

<a id="u01486"></a>

one trains the expressiveness of the yogic ordinary mind.

<a id="u01487"></a>

The crucial point is familiarity with sleep.

<a id="u01488"></a>

Through the four times of suchness, [N-071](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-071)

<a id="u01489"></a>

definitely gathering body, speech, and ordinary mind,

<a id="u01490"></a>

one mixes great clear light with sleep.

<a id="u01491"></a>

Having become familiar in this way, the yogin

<a id="u01492"></a>

definitely joins [this] to the three embodiments.

<a id="u01493"></a>

Taking the crucial points of dreams onto the path

<a id="u01494"></a>

consists of preparation and applying the crucial points.

<a id="u01495"></a>

First training body, speech, and ordinary mind,

<a id="u01496"></a>

and relying on the signs of proficiency,

<a id="u01497"></a>

examination and overpowering, and

<a id="u01498"></a>

habitual tendencies—the three—are definitely to be retained. [N-072](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-072)

<a id="u01499"></a>

Then, at the time of applying the crucial points:

<a id="u01500"></a>

training, transformation, cutting off, and

<a id="u01501"></a>

stirring, concentrating the vitality, and releasing at the crucial point;

<a id="u01502"></a>

by applying the crucial points of gathering and reversing, [N-072](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-072)

<a id="u01503"></a>

karmic dreams are exhausted to their limit,

<a id="u01504"></a>

and the delusion of habitual tendencies is uprooted.

<a id="u01505"></a>

At this time, through one's own diligence,

<a id="u01506"></a>

for the superior they cease; for the middling they are known;

<a id="u01507"></a>

for the inferior they definitely change.

<a id="u01508"></a>

As for the beginning of these dreams, too:

<a id="u01509"></a>

for the superior they are forgotten, and for the inferior they cease;

<a id="u01510"></a>

for the middling they are exceedingly clear,

<a id="u01511"></a>

and the inferior knows that [they are dreams]. [N-072](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-072)

<a id="u01512"></a>

For the inferior they are unclear, then change.

<a id="u01513"></a>

Through these, the measure [of proficiency] is reached.

<a id="u01514"></a>

**[Source heading: Thirty-first reply.]**

<a id="u01515"></a>

A person's own ordinary mind, when untamed,

<a id="u01516"></a>

is definitely tamed by cultivating non-conceptuality.

<a id="u01517"></a>

Having settled into its own condition, it is one's own. [N-073](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-073)

<a id="u01518"></a>

The sequence of prior training

<a id="u01519"></a>

and the main practice depend on one's own continuum.

<a id="u01520"></a>

The sequence of prior training is this:

<a id="u01521"></a>

in the preliminary practice of the body,

<a id="u01522"></a>

the causes of the respective activities are stopped.[^G-U01522]

<a id="u01523"></a>

Assuming the vajra cross-legged posture,

<a id="u01524"></a>

one trains the body, benefiting the ordinary mind.

<a id="u01525"></a>

For speech, abiding in the syllable HŪṂ,

<a id="u01526"></a>

one applies the seal and trains expressiveness;

<a id="u01527"></a>

by entering the path of seeking the formidable, [N-073](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-073)

<a id="u01528"></a>

one thus trains speech, benefiting the ordinary mind.

<a id="u01529"></a>

For the ordinary mind: where it initially arises,

<a id="u01530"></a>

where it abides in between, and where it finally goes—

<a id="u01531"></a>

by examining and investigating these three,

<a id="u01532"></a>

one trains the ordinary mind and knows its natural state.

<a id="u01533"></a>

The main practice, relying on one's own continuum,

<a id="u01534"></a>

depends on the crucial points of body and speech[^G-U01534]

<a id="u01535"></a>

and the crucial point of ordinary mind.

<a id="u01536"></a>

Wind is to be made slow by every means.

<a id="u01537"></a>

Arising from thorough projection, [N-073](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-073)

<a id="u01538"></a>

the connection between body and ordinary mind is cut;

<a id="u01539"></a>

there is no turning back into the three realms.[^G-U01539]

<a id="u01540"></a>

**[Source heading: Thirty-second reply.]**[^G-U01540]

<a id="u01541"></a>

Wind has two kinds of purpose.

<a id="u01542"></a>

In the world, it generates the complete body,

<a id="u01543"></a>

and [supports] the lifespan through which it is sustained and abides;

<a id="u01544"></a>

it increases power, lifespan, and so forth.

<a id="u01545"></a>

It performs the activities of merit and karma;

<a id="u01546"></a>

it generates beings' luster and splendor;

<a id="u01547"></a>

it performs every activity of spreading and gathering.

<a id="u01548"></a>

For the transcendent: embodiments, primordial knowing,

<a id="u01549"></a>

and familiarity with spheres themselves;

<a id="u01550"></a>

pervasion that is light, empty, and clear; [N-074](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-074)

<a id="u01551"></a>

the exhaustion of the body's elements;

<a id="u01552"></a>

the stopping of the ordinary mind's conceptual thought;

<a id="u01553"></a>

the arising of non-conceptual primordial knowing;

<a id="u01554"></a>

the exhaustion of outer appearances;

<a id="u01555"></a>

and the mixing of wind and ordinary mind, the two. [N-074](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-074)

<a id="u01556"></a>

Thus wind performs all activities.

<a id="u01557"></a>

**[Source heading: Thirty-third reply.]**[^G-U01557]

<a id="u01558"></a>

The crucial point of the body is the channels:

<a id="u01559"></a>

their places, arrangement, and movement;

<a id="u01560"></a>

their support of the wheels and the body's life-force;

<a id="u01561"></a>

names, particulars, causes, and conditions;

<a id="u01562"></a>

the activities they perform and their characteristics;

<a id="u01563"></a>

afflictive activities and activities of primordial knowing;

<a id="u01564"></a>

manifest appearance through reliance on the path;

<a id="u01565"></a>

illnesses and the particulars of the elements—

<a id="u01566"></a>

they are distinguished by the extent of their abiding in confusion. [N-075](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-075)

<a id="u01567"></a>

Constricting stops; wringing binds. [N-075](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-075)[^G-U01567]

<a id="u01568"></a>

Pressing makes [them] abide; squeezing makes [them] clear.

<a id="u01569"></a>

Twisting makes [them] cross; drawing makes [them] known.

<a id="u01570"></a>

Pushing makes [them] stay; forcefulness makes [them] held. [N-075](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-075)

<a id="u01571"></a>

Thus, through the crucial points of the channels, too,

<a id="u01572"></a>

the abode of buddhahood is indicated.

<a id="u01573"></a>

The winds that depend on the channels

<a id="u01574"></a>

move twenty thousand, one thousand,

<a id="u01575"></a>

and six hundred [times].

<a id="u01576"></a>

These are reckoned as the winds of great movement.

<a id="u01577"></a>

Their activity has two aspects:

<a id="u01578"></a>

moving upward and clearing downward.

<a id="u01579"></a>

Or, when a skilled person seeks the crucial point,

<a id="u01580"></a>

those definitely moving from the channels—

<a id="u01581"></a>

one hundred thousand, twenty thousand,

<a id="u01582"></a>

six thousand, and six hundred—

<a id="u01583"></a>

definitely arise from the channels.

<a id="u01584"></a>

They are reckoned as the winds of small movement. [N-075](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-075)

<a id="u01585"></a>

Times of illness, afflicting spirits, and lifespan

<a id="u01586"></a>

are to be known by the skilled person.

<a id="u01587"></a>

Through their crucial point, one strives in methods of application.

<a id="u01588"></a>

For the great [movements], one calculates the time

<a id="u01589"></a>

and examines supreme and common accomplishments.

<a id="u01590"></a>

Through the particulars of their respective activities,

<a id="u01591"></a>

between karma and primordial knowing, the two,

<a id="u01592"></a>

and among afflictions and freedom from conceptualization,

<a id="u01593"></a>

one takes the respective numerical measures. [N-075](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-075)

<a id="u01594"></a>

The cultivator trains thoroughly;

<a id="u01595"></a>

through this the measures of the respective winds are grasped.[^G-U01595]

<a id="u01596"></a>

**[Source heading: Thirty-fourth reply.]**

<a id="u01597"></a>

One wishing to stabilize the nature of phenomena of the sphere: [N-076](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-076)

<a id="u01598"></a>

there are the ultimate and the relative.[^G-U01598]

<a id="u01599"></a>

For now, relying on the relative sphere,

<a id="u01600"></a>

one wishing for buddhahood—

<a id="u01601"></a>

a woman of the family, with complete characteristics,

<a id="u01602"></a>

from gods, demigods, or Brahmā's [class],

<a id="u01603"></a>

or even a low-caste non-Buddhist—[N-076](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-076)

<a id="u01604"></a>

if one sees [her] complete characteristics,

<a id="u01605"></a>

one definitely undertakes the application of summoning.

<a id="u01606"></a>

Through what is to be relied upon, the channels,

<a id="u01607"></a>

and the objects of focus, one completes the body. [N-076](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-076)

<a id="u01608"></a>

Then the relative sphere itself

<a id="u01609"></a>

is lowered, retained, and reversed;

<a id="u01610"></a>

it is placed in the channels and mixed with wind.

<a id="u01611"></a>

Emptiness is to be stabilized; the traces of conceptual mind are cut.

<a id="u01612"></a>

Body and ordinary mind, the two, are to exchange their positions. [N-076](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-076)

<a id="u01613"></a>

Through stabilizing the ultimate sphere,

<a id="u01614"></a>

one finds the empty domains of the dharma embodiment.

<a id="u01615"></a>

Packing the lamp of the empty sphere itself,

<a id="u01616"></a>

one trains the expressiveness of awareness. [N-076](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-076)

<a id="u01617"></a>

Engaging in familiarity without [distinguishing] day and night,

<a id="u01618"></a>

this itself appears directly, free from effort.

<a id="u01619"></a>

This is the measure of proficiency.

<a id="u01620"></a>

**[Source heading: Thirty-fifth reply.]**

<a id="u01621"></a>

The extent of the activities of ḍākinīs,

<a id="u01622"></a>

if differentiated, cannot be encompassed by expression;

<a id="u01623"></a>

nevertheless, it will be explained in brief.

<a id="u01624"></a>

Through profound dependent connections,

<a id="u01625"></a>

one is free of food, clothing, and so forth,

<a id="u01626"></a>

attains uncontaminated great bliss,

<a id="u01627"></a>

and gains mastery over the four elements. [N-077](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-077)[^G-U01627]

<a id="u01628"></a>

Through the culmination of activities examined in actuality,

<a id="u01629"></a>

and through examining the domains of form, sound, smell, taste,

<a id="u01630"></a>

and touch, [N-T03](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t03)

<a id="u01631"></a>

the culmination of common worldly activities

<a id="u01632"></a>

and higher knowing are perfected,

<a id="u01633"></a>

and one enjoys the fields.

<a id="u01634"></a>

**[Source heading: Thirty-sixth reply.]**[^G-U01634]

<a id="u01635"></a>

There are three particular kinds of transference, [N-T32](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t32)[^G-U01635]

<a id="u01636"></a>

according to distinctions in faculties:

<a id="u01637"></a>

clear light and illusory body;

<a id="u01638"></a>

for the last, body, speech, and mental faculty. [N-077](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-077)

<a id="u01639"></a>

Transference of body and speech has two aspects:

<a id="u01640"></a>

actual transference and nonactual transference.

<a id="u01641"></a>

The actual is a method of training through wind.

<a id="u01642"></a>

Through training in word, craft, illusory expressiveness,

<a id="u01643"></a>

and dependent connections,

<a id="u01644"></a>

when the respective elements accord inwardly,

<a id="u01645"></a>

it is accomplished by directing body, speech, and mental faculty. [N-077](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-077)

<a id="u01646"></a>

For the nonactual, stabilize the power of the ordinary mind's familiarity.

<a id="u01647"></a>

Train the illusory body in dreams.

<a id="u01648"></a>

Through proficiency in this, the body itself

<a id="u01649"></a>

becomes like the form of a shadow.

<a id="u01650"></a>

Through this, the body of the intermediate state

<a id="u01651"></a>

comes to be held within this condition. [N-077](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-077)

<a id="u01652"></a>

Clear-light transference is for the superior:

<a id="u01653"></a>

having trained in the meditative stabilization of sleep,

<a id="u01654"></a>

through proficiency, without [distinguishing] day and night,

<a id="u01655"></a>

when outer appearances are clear,

<a id="u01656"></a>

through this great clear light is accomplished.

<a id="u01657"></a>

Its body, too, is threefold. [N-077](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-077)[^G-U01657]

<a id="u01658"></a>

Through the stages of diligence,

<a id="u01659"></a>

into the emanation embodiment, complete enjoyment embodiment, and dharma embodiment,

<a id="u01660"></a>

one can definitely transfer in actuality.

<a id="u01661"></a>

**[Source heading: Thirty-seventh reply.]**[^G-U01661]

<a id="u01662"></a>

The application of dependent connections has two aspects:

<a id="u01663"></a>

accomplishing manifold miracles

<a id="u01664"></a>

and the rites of the crucial points of the elements.

<a id="u01665"></a>

Manifold miracles have manifold methods.

<a id="u01666"></a>

Grasping the various paths, one applies one's own knowing. [N-078](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-078)

<a id="u01667"></a>

The moving and unmoving are encircled with wind.

<a id="u01668"></a>

*Source annotation moved to endnotes; no root verse at U01668.*[^G-U01668]

<a id="u01669"></a>

They are differentiated through accomplishing body and faculties.

<a id="u01670"></a>

The crucial points of the elements definitely produce

<a id="u01671"></a>

beneficial and harmful results for oneself and others.

<a id="u01672"></a>

Join [them] with each person's body and elements:

<a id="u01673"></a>

water, earth, fire, and wind.

<a id="u01674"></a>

Through the dependent connections of their respective activities,

<a id="u01675"></a>

one restores, increases, or diminishes [them].

<a id="u01676"></a>

Earth and water destroy;

<a id="u01677"></a>

earth and fire increase;

<a id="u01678"></a>

earth and wind bring down a slayer;

<a id="u01679"></a>

water and fire cause decline;

<a id="u01680"></a>

water and wind restore;

<a id="u01681"></a>

fire and wind equalize;

<a id="u01682"></a>

the four gathered together produce confusion. [N-078](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-078)

<a id="u01683"></a>

Whatever dependent connections are expressed,

<a id="u01684"></a>

relate them to each yogin's experience.

<a id="u01685"></a>

For the mental faculty's dependent connections, inquire of wind. [N-078](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-078)

<a id="u01686"></a>

As for the activity of the three gathered together,

<a id="u01687"></a>

when properly combined with each corresponding arrangement,

<a id="u01688"></a>

through this, too, benefit and harm are accomplished.[^G-U01688]

<a id="u01689"></a>

**[Source heading: Thirty-eighth reply.]**

<a id="u01690"></a>

One wishing to be liberated from illness and suffering

<a id="u01691"></a>

applies [the methods] distinctly to oneself and others.

<a id="u01692"></a>

For others, act through dependent connections;

<a id="u01693"></a>

for oneself, perform transferring and stroking. [N-079](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-079)

<a id="u01694"></a>

There are three actions:

<a id="u01695"></a>

through the crucial point of body, speech,

<a id="u01696"></a>

and likewise the abiding of ordinary mind, one is liberated.

<a id="u01697"></a>

For the body, in the various bodily mechanisms:

<a id="u01698"></a>

the lion and elephant,

<a id="u01699"></a>

vulture and sheep shake [the body];

<a id="u01700"></a>

the vixen and the person,

<a id="u01701"></a>

worms and so forth—with bodily [forms]

<a id="u01702"></a>

and manifold bodily mechanisms,

<a id="u01703"></a>

outer and inner, upper and lower, limbs and body,

<a id="u01704"></a>

wind, bile, phlegm, and their conjunction,

<a id="u01705"></a>

are combined with the four groups of elemental illnesses. [N-079](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-079)

<a id="u01706"></a>

Distinguishing their extremes as hot and cold,

<a id="u01707"></a>

one joins each with its counterpart and is liberated from illness.

<a id="u01708"></a>

For speech, there are manifold sounds and voices:

<a id="u01709"></a>

with āli and kāli exceedingly clear,

<a id="u01710"></a>

for the letters abiding by intrinsic nature in the channels,

<a id="u01711"></a>

through the six ways of reversing [them], [N-079](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-079)[^G-U01711]

<a id="u01712"></a>

one joins [them] to their respective places.

<a id="u01713"></a>

The voices of gods, nāgas, and yakṣas,

<a id="u01714"></a>

rākṣasas and sky-soarers;

<a id="u01715"></a>

Brahmā, the Hundred-Giver, and the All-Pervader;

<a id="u01716"></a>

spirits and wind gods; [N-079](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-079)[^G-U01716]

<a id="u01717"></a>

kumbhāṇḍas, belly-crawlers, and gandharvas,

<a id="u01718"></a>

and the voices of further entities—[N-079](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-079)

<a id="u01719"></a>

by joining the respective voices, one is liberated.

<a id="u01720"></a>

For ordinary mind, manifold forms of mindfulness,

<a id="u01721"></a>

and the extent of whatever movement occurs,

<a id="u01722"></a>

are distinguished through the yogin's awareness.

<a id="u01723"></a>

Furthermore, transform through body, speech, and mental faculty.

<a id="u01724"></a>

Or, through the activity of ordinary mind's doing,

<a id="u01725"></a>

transform through manifold deep absorptions,

<a id="u01726"></a>

and distinguish the extent of suffering as well. [N-079](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-079)

<a id="u01727"></a>

**[Source heading: Thirty-ninth reply.]**[^G-U01727]

<a id="u01728"></a>

The shifting of the elements is like this:

<a id="u01729"></a>

Ema! It is explained as greatly wondrous.

<a id="u01730"></a>

Through the distinction of outer and inner,

<a id="u01731"></a>

earth [joins] earth; the remaining [elements], too,

<a id="u01732"></a>

are to be understood by extension from the preceding [account].

<a id="u01733"></a>

Day and night, morning and evening, days,

<a id="u01734"></a>

months and years, through the manner of error— [N-080](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-080)[^G-U01734]

<a id="u01735"></a>

sets of twelve, and the coarse [divisions], too;

<a id="u01736"></a>

penetration, dispersal, and equalizing,

<a id="u01737"></a>

and abiding indeterminately in confusion—the four—[N-080](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-080)

<a id="u01738"></a>

are definitely distinguished by time: sixteen.

<a id="u01739"></a>

Their respective definite times, the intervals of shifting,

<a id="u01740"></a>

and the places of the elements in dependent connections and so forth:

<a id="u01741"></a>

at the time of shifting, there is shifting;

<a id="u01742"></a>

likewise, at the time of entering, entering;

<a id="u01743"></a>

at the time of abiding, abiding;

<a id="u01744"></a>

and in confusion itself, confusion.

<a id="u01745"></a>

These concern day and night, days and months.

<a id="u01746"></a>

The places of shifting are examined by the skilled person.

<a id="u01747"></a>

Through water, earth, fire, and wind,

<a id="u01748"></a>

concordance and discordance,

<a id="u01749"></a>

and the respective combinations of activities and times,

<a id="u01750"></a>

the culminations of activities are to be transformed. [N-080](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-080)

<a id="u01751"></a>

**[Source heading: Fortieth reply.]**

<a id="u01752"></a>

Distinguishing the elements of the crucial points:

<a id="u01753"></a>

from four hundred and four,

<a id="u01754"></a>

the specified entries are condensed into eighty;

<a id="u01755"></a>

from these, twenty-four;

<a id="u01756"></a>

the supreme abidings themselves are sixteen;

<a id="u01757"></a>

through male–female pairing, they become eight.[^G-U01757]

<a id="u01758"></a>

Through four pure in their own identity,

<a id="u01759"></a>

the crucial points are gathered and definitely taught. [N-081](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-081)

<a id="u01760"></a>

When the activities of dispersal, penetration, and equalizing

<a id="u01761"></a>

are joined to dates, months, and years

<a id="u01762"></a>

without error,

<a id="u01763"></a>

the crucial points, too, become definite.

<a id="u01764"></a>

**[Source heading: Forty-first reply.]**

<a id="u01765"></a>

Thus, relying on the crucial points,

<a id="u01766"></a>

the branches of the application of calculation are explained.

<a id="u01767"></a>

Ema! How greatly wondrous!

<a id="u01768"></a>

Not spoken by other buddhas,

<a id="u01769"></a>

the sequence of elemental calculation—

<a id="u01770"></a>

relying on which one measures samsara and nirvana—

<a id="u01771"></a>

listen to this utmost secret! [N-082](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-082)

<a id="u01772"></a>

From water, earth, fire, and wind,

<a id="u01773"></a>

there is a sequence of differentiation.

<a id="u01774"></a>

The fifth one is neuter and empty.

<a id="u01775"></a>

This is the sequence of knowing and calculating.

<a id="u01776"></a>

*Source annotation moved to endnotes; no root verse at U01776.*[^G-U01776]

<a id="u01777"></a>

From the twelve activities

<a id="u01778"></a>

of penetration, dispersal, and equalizing, the mother-years, too,

<a id="u01779"></a>

number twelve; in groups of three,

<a id="u01780"></a>

the respective objects of focus and dependent connections, too,

<a id="u01781"></a>

are examined by the skilled person in their application to times.

<a id="u01782"></a>

Za-ma, days, months, and years—[N-082](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-082)

<a id="u01783"></a>

through the times of dispersal, penetration, and equalizing,

<a id="u01784"></a>

one grasps the times of birth, aging, illness,

<a id="u01785"></a>

and likewise death.

<a id="u01786"></a>

Planets, stars, and afflicting spirits,

<a id="u01787"></a>

and the sequence of the sun's times:

<a id="u01788"></a>

calculate their particular favorable and unfavorable aspects.

<a id="u01789"></a>

The skilled person grasps what should and should not be done.

<a id="u01790"></a>

Through form, sound, smell, and taste, [N-T03](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t03)

<a id="u01791"></a>

examine the karmic connections of earlier and later lives.

<a id="u01792"></a>

Through what moves the respective elements,

<a id="u01793"></a>

grasp the growth of virtuous application and the times of afflicting spirits.

<a id="u01794"></a>

Through sixteen inwardly concordant distinctions,

<a id="u01795"></a>

examine karma, afflictions, and primordial knowing.

<a id="u01796"></a>

The elements generate, abide, and destroy;

<a id="u01797"></a>

empty, wander, and spread without limit;

<a id="u01798"></a>

coil, revolve, and turn back;

<a id="u01799"></a>

become sharp, sluggish, fierce, and weak;

<a id="u01800"></a>

and [act as] enemy, slayer, and friend. [N-082](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-082)

<a id="u01801"></a>

Through these, calculate the categories of each respective activity.

<a id="u01802"></a>

Through the forms of the outer elements,

<a id="u01803"></a>

taking ya bzhi [ཡ་བཞི་; sense unresolved], bring each into accord. [N-083](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-083)[^G-U01803]

<a id="u01804"></a>

Through the respective combinations of their elements,

<a id="u01805"></a>

grasp the times of lifespan and ascendancy.

<a id="u01806"></a>

*Source annotation moved to endnotes; no root verse at U01806.*[^G-U01806]

<a id="u01807"></a>

Birth, illness, and death

<a id="u01808"></a>

are definitely related to oneself and others.

<a id="u01809"></a>

For the months of winter, summer, spring, and autumn,

<a id="u01810"></a>

counting three [for each] makes twelve.

<a id="u01811"></a>

The days of a thousand years, too, number three hundred thousand. [N-083](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-083)[^G-U01811]

<a id="u01812"></a>

*Joined source fragment; no additional root text at U01812.*[^G-U01812][^G-U01811]

<a id="u01813"></a>

Through the stars' not being two,

<a id="u01814"></a>

years and months are piled one upon another. [N-083](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-083)

<a id="u01815"></a>

Through what transforms day and night,

<a id="u01816"></a>

there are two times when day and night are equal.

<a id="u01817"></a>

Among the categories known as dates,

<a id="u01818"></a>

in the ascending half, nights are long.

<a id="u01819"></a>

Then, by one hundred and eighty,

<a id="u01820"></a>

in the descending half, days are long. [N-083](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-083)

<a id="u01821"></a>

For making day and night long or short,

<a id="u01822"></a>

through distinguishing the means of male and female elements,

<a id="u01823"></a>

from the twelvefold sequence of day and night,

<a id="u01824"></a>

the skilled person grasps the times of reversal.

<a id="u01825"></a>

Through the length and shortness of day and night,

<a id="u01826"></a>

virtue and nonvirtue are distinguished.

<a id="u01827"></a>

For days, too, by extension from day and night,

<a id="u01828"></a>

months and years: dispersal, penetration, and equalizing.

<a id="u01829"></a>

*Source annotation moved to endnotes; no root verse at U01829.*[^G-U01829]

<a id="u01830"></a>

Join the twelve times of dependent connections.

<a id="u01831"></a>

Knowing all twelve in sequence,

<a id="u01832"></a>

one completes Mañjuśrī's knowing.

<a id="u01833"></a>

Through the magical displays of the outer elements

<a id="u01834"></a>

and the categories of long and short days and nights,

<a id="u01835"></a>

join the elemental years to the measures of time.

<a id="u01836"></a>

For years, apply the respective extensions;

<a id="u01837"></a>

at the outset, join their own times equally.

<a id="u01838"></a>

Wind, light, and great sound;

<a id="u01839"></a>

earthquakes, evenness, and reflections;

<a id="u01840"></a>

and the manner in which [they] vanish into space—

<a id="u01841"></a>

definitely relating these to their respective directions,

<a id="u01842"></a>

one knows frost, heavy rain, and absence of rain;

<a id="u01843"></a>

famine, illness, and classes of afflicting spirits;

<a id="u01844"></a>

conflict and movement of ‘mountain-throne’ (ri khri), [N-084](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-084)

<a id="u01845"></a>

through their respective times and classes.

<a id="u01846"></a>

Kings, common people, and spiritual practitioners;

<a id="u01847"></a>

queens, ministers, and paṇḍitas;

<a id="u01848"></a>

the old, the young, and livestock;

<a id="u01849"></a>

further, the particulars of the various animals;

<a id="u01850"></a>

the forms of men and women;

<a id="u01851"></a>

fruit harvests, field crops, and medicinal harvests—

<a id="u01852"></a>

their success and failure, favorable and unfavorable aspects,

<a id="u01853"></a>

are to be known by the yogin in sequence. [N-084](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-084)

<a id="u01854"></a>

**[Source heading: Forty-second reply.]**

<a id="u01855"></a>

Ema! Wondrous and marvelous,

<a id="u01856"></a>

this bliss of the elements is like this:

<a id="u01857"></a>

not known in other teachings,

<a id="u01858"></a>

utterly secret and unsurpassed.

<a id="u01859"></a>

Because you, fortunate one, command great regard,

<a id="u01860"></a>

unable to refuse [you], I explain it to you. [N-085](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-085)

<a id="u01861"></a>

The supremely fortunate, diligent person,

<a id="u01862"></a>

free from the mental faculty [turned toward] samsara,

<a id="u01863"></a>

should offer to and praise the guru.

<a id="u01864"></a>

Abandoning all worldly activities,

<a id="u01865"></a>

accord with the respective elements:

<a id="u01866"></a>

water, earth, fire, and wind.

<a id="u01867"></a>

Definitely relate each to its respective body.

<a id="u01868"></a>

Through the order of the respective elements,

<a id="u01869"></a>

definitely examine the forms of entities.

<a id="u01870"></a>

Regarding form, sound, smell, and taste,

<a id="u01871"></a>

and the qualities of their respective desires,

<a id="u01872"></a>

one should train, beginning with sound. [N-085](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-085) [N-T03](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t03)

<a id="u01873"></a>

Furthermore, the fortunate yogin

<a id="u01874"></a>

properly examines and investigates the colors

<a id="u01875"></a>

and shapes of the respective entities,

<a id="u01876"></a>

and applies [them] to common and supreme accomplishment.

<a id="u01877"></a>

Then apply [this] to their respective sounds.

<a id="u01878"></a>

Through definite proficiency in sound,

<a id="u01879"></a>

undertaking the remainder brings accomplishment.

<a id="u01880"></a>

Thus, concerning the outer elements,

<a id="u01881"></a>

through superior, middling, and inferior diligence,

<a id="u01882"></a>

in days, months, and years,

<a id="a2000-c01-s02"></a>

*Scan-restored main verses:*[^G-A2000-C01-S02]

At their respective measures, [they] are accomplished through signs.<br />
Thus, regarding the accomplishment of embodiment:<br />
the emanation embodiment and complete enjoyment embodiment,

<a id="u01883"></a>

the dharma embodiment and the embodiment of essence itself—[^G-U01883]

<a id="u01884"></a>

[one] joins [them] as the supreme result of embodiment. [N-085](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-085)[^G-U01884]

<a id="u01885"></a>

For one is neuter and empty.

<a id="u01886"></a>

The distinctions of sound and so forth

<a id="u01887"></a>

depend on the respective particulars

<a id="u01888"></a>

of the times of abiding, entering, arising, and reversing.

<a id="u01889"></a>

For the application of common activities,

<a id="u01890"></a>

relying on the voices of ḍākinīs, the six classes,

<a id="u01891"></a>

and the respective elements,

<a id="u01892"></a>

one accomplishes higher knowing.

<a id="u01893"></a>

Free of food and clothing, and, regarding the elements,

<a id="u01894"></a>

free from sound, one confers empowerment. [N-085](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-085)

<a id="u01895"></a>

The body's elements are exhausted,

<a id="u01896"></a>

and [the body] becomes like the shadow of a lamp.

<a id="u01897"></a>

Thoroughly free from the sufferings of aging and death,

<a id="u01898"></a>

one becomes equal to the sun and moon.

<a id="u01899"></a>

Having cut the flow of outer and inner breath, [Editorial note: Do not attempt breath cessation from this draft.] [N-085](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-085)

<a id="u01900"></a>

all differentiating conceptualization is stopped.

<a id="u01901"></a>

Relying on non-conceptual meditative stabilization,

<a id="u01902"></a>

one closely joins the basic space of phenomena.

<a id="u01903"></a>

Having assumed a common worldly body,

<a id="u01904"></a>

when this itself is exhausted,

<a id="u01905"></a>

there is no return to the three realms.

<a id="u01906"></a>

**[Source heading: Forty-third reply.]**

<a id="u01907"></a>

The activity of transferring has two aspects:

<a id="u01908"></a>

entities themselves and emptiness.

<a id="u01909"></a>

Entities are included within form.

<a id="u01910"></a>

When one is thus proficient in the elements,

<a id="u01911"></a>

having stabilized familiarity with wind,

<a id="u01912"></a>

one can make any entity possessing form move

<a id="u01913"></a>

by placing knowing within it.

<a id="u01914"></a>

This is merely transferring into an entity. [N-086](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-086)

<a id="u01915"></a>

For emptiness, familiarity of ordinary mind is primary.

<a id="u01916"></a>

By joining the basic space of phenomena and the dharma embodiment

<a id="u01917"></a>

nondually, one knows [them] as empty.

<a id="u01918"></a>

In emptiness, whose intrinsic nature is clarity,

<a id="u01919"></a>

ordinary mind, too, is in that condition.

<a id="u01920"></a>

Through completion of the rite of dependent connections,

<a id="u01921"></a>

For transferring at the crucial point, the method of releasing

<a id="u01922"></a>

and the utterance of voices—through these, one should know. [N-086](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-086)

<a id="u01923"></a>

Through the meeting and gathering of the respective elements,

<a id="u01924"></a>

one is to transfer through seeing alone.

<a id="u01925"></a>

On seeing the form of an animal,

<a id="u01926"></a>

the skilled person joins [this] to wind.

<a id="u01927"></a>

But when accomplishment is reached,

<a id="u01928"></a>

through an invisible form all bodies are accomplished. [N-086](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-086)

<a id="u01929"></a>

On seeing material substances to which one is attached,

<a id="u01930"></a>

take [them] by transferring, to offer to the guru.

<a id="u01931"></a>

It is not to be done otherwise.

<a id="u01932"></a>

Likewise, youthful forms and so forth: [N-086](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-086)

<a id="u01933"></a>

when the guru, the hero, commands,

<a id="u01934"></a>

through familiarity with wind, take these too.

<a id="u01935"></a>

Possessing an ordinary mind that enters,

<a id="u01936"></a>

the yogin should transfer. [N-086](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-086)

<a id="u01937"></a>

**[Source heading: Forty-fourth reply.]**

<a id="u01938"></a>

A greatly fortunate yogin wishing to transfer,

<a id="u01939"></a>

within what is gathered by ordinary mind, together with its family,

<a id="u01940"></a>

to living beings appearing in form,

<a id="u01941"></a>

through inwardly concordant transformations of activity,

<a id="u01942"></a>

transfers his own illness and so forth to others.

<a id="u01943"></a>

By transferring in this way, one is released from the actual [illness]. [N-087](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-087)

<a id="u01944"></a>

Further, the application of dependent connections is taught.

<a id="u01945"></a>

From the four elements of insentient matter,

<a id="u01946"></a>

through dependent connections that transform entities,

<a id="u01947"></a>

with numbers, signs, and voices,

<a id="u01948"></a>

casting the object of focus, one transfers illness and so forth.

<a id="u01949"></a>

Sticks, pebbles, and grit;

<a id="u01950"></a>

grass, fragments of firebrands, potsherds, and so forth;

<a id="u01951"></a>

likewise, water, earth, and space—

<a id="u01952"></a>

definitely taking these up, one transforms the activity of transferring

<a id="u01953"></a>

through the power of the yogin's ordinary mind.

<a id="u01954"></a>

The skilled person examines its signs and measures:

<a id="u01955"></a>

what has been placed is broken and turned,

<a id="u01956"></a>

raised, laid down, and straightened;

<a id="u01957"></a>

shifted, lifted, and discarded;

<a id="u01958"></a>

held, wiped, and struck.

<a id="u01959"></a>

Through these, the practitioner should know thoroughly. [N-087](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-087)

<a id="u01960"></a>

Possessing the gesture of summoning,

<a id="u01961"></a>

food and wealth bound by miserliness

<a id="u01962"></a>

are taken by transferring, to offer to the guru.

<a id="u01963"></a>

When this itself is clear as an object of focus in ordinary mind,

<a id="u01964"></a>

like iron gathering to a lodestone,

<a id="u01965"></a>

one definitely takes [them].

<a id="u01966"></a>

But when undertaken for one's own worldly desires,

<a id="u01967"></a>

it is not accomplished. [N-087](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-087)

<a id="u01968"></a>

**[Source heading: Forty-fifth reply.]**

<a id="u01969"></a>

Not explained in any other continuum:

<a id="u01970"></a>

the elements' crucial points—drawing forth and reversing.

<a id="u01971"></a>

I will definitely explain their profundity; listen.

<a id="u01972"></a>

Wind, bile, and phlegm;

<a id="u01973"></a>

the conjunction of two and the conjunction of three;

<a id="u01974"></a>

their halves and twelve parts—[N-088](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-088)

<a id="u01975"></a>

definitely relating their sequence to the times,

<a id="u01976"></a>

for two causes and eight conditions,

<a id="u01977"></a>

identify hot and cold.

<a id="u01978"></a>

Join the outer and inner domains as pairs.

<a id="u01979"></a>

The skilled person distinguishes body and limbs,

<a id="u01980"></a>

relating the upper, lower, and middle places.

<a id="u01981"></a>

Possessing the three places, one draws out illnesses. [N-088](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-088)

<a id="u01982"></a>

Through the body's manifold bodily mechanisms

<a id="u01983"></a>

and the culmination of activities of restraining through vital essences,

<a id="u01984"></a>

draw all elemental illnesses outward.

<a id="u01985"></a>

Definitely relating body and limbs, outer and inner,

<a id="u01986"></a>

one should turn ordinary mind back. [N-088](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-088)

<a id="u01987"></a>

For speech, act through manifold sounds and voices,

<a id="u01988"></a>

manifold combinations of letter-signs,

<a id="u01989"></a>

abiding, and the meaning beyond expression.

<a id="u01990"></a>

For ordinary mind, relate earth, water, fire, and wind,

<a id="u01991"></a>

and manifold objects of focus, to the illness.

<a id="u01992"></a>

Reversal is through the crucial points of the faculties.

<a id="u01993"></a>

Through the respective numbers and measures,

<a id="u01994"></a>

definite signs, measures, and the interval of liberation—

<a id="u01995"></a>

by definitely knowing their measures of time,

<a id="u01996"></a>

one's own body is liberated from illness,

<a id="u01997"></a>

and one knows the application of dependent connections for others' bodies.

<a id="u01998"></a>

By acting in this way, the purpose is accomplished. [N-088](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-088)

<a id="u01999"></a>

**[Source heading: Forty-sixth reply.]**

<a id="u02000"></a>

The enlightened intent of tenet systems has two aspects: [N-089](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-089)

<a id="u02001"></a>

the enlightened intent for entering the worldly path,

<a id="u02002"></a>

and the enlightened intent of the ultimate meaning.

<a id="u02003"></a>

Through their respective Grounds and faculties,

<a id="u02004"></a>

the worldly and the transcendent

<a id="u02005"></a>

are held to be two vehicles.

<a id="a2000-c01-s03"></a>

*Scan-restored main verses:*[^G-A2000-C01-S03]

The Ground is worldly;<br />
from this, there are two assertions.<br />
The transcendent, in terms of cause and result—

<a id="u02006"></a>

For the cause there are three; for the result, two.

<a id="u02007"></a>

Dividing the latter two gives six.

<a id="u02008"></a>

This is according to the sequence of faculties.

<a id="u02009"></a>

Thus, relying on nine,

<a id="u02010"></a>

the three realms, too, are made to enter

<a id="u02011"></a>

the authentic path. [N-090](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-090)

<a id="u02012"></a>

The ultimate meaning is free from conceptual mind.

<a id="u02013"></a>

Through self-liberation for faculties of the simultaneous type,

<a id="u02014"></a>

and through the crucial point of seeing in direct perception,

<a id="u02015"></a>

the limits of one's own tenet system dissolve.

<a id="u02016"></a>

Without attachment or holding to anything,

<a id="u02017"></a>

having tasted the authentic nature of phenomena,

<a id="u02018"></a>

without a support for the samsara of the three realms,

<a id="u02019"></a>

like space dissolving into space itself,

<a id="u02020"></a>

the supreme yogin, too, is like that. [N-090](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-090)

<a id="u02021"></a>

**[Source heading: Forty-seventh reply.]**

<a id="u02022"></a>

The enlightened intent of the ultimate is this.

<a id="u02023"></a>

The culmination of generation is the body.

<a id="u02024"></a>

Through its union with the deity,

<a id="u02025"></a>

it culminates in the exhaustion of entities.

<a id="u02026"></a>

The culmination of the completion stage itself:

<a id="u02027"></a>

because there is movement and holding,

<a id="u02028"></a>

wind and knowing are united.

<a id="u02029"></a>

It culminates in the exhaustion of appearances.

<a id="u02030"></a>

For the subject expressed by a tenet system,

<a id="u02031"></a>

through uniting one's own and others' assertions,

<a id="u02032"></a>

it culminates in knowing one's own identity.

<a id="u02033"></a>

The culmination of Mahamudra itself:

<a id="u02034"></a>

transformation is bliss.

<a id="u02035"></a>

Through the union of bliss and the empty,

<a id="u02036"></a>

it culminates in the absence of mental engagement. [N-091](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-091)

<a id="u02037"></a>

The culmination of the Great Perfection of intrinsic nature:

<a id="u02038"></a>

phenomena are left in their own places.

<a id="u02039"></a>

Through uniting appearance and knowing with the nature of phenomena,

<a id="u02040"></a>

it culminates in leaving primordial liberation just as it is. [N-091](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-091)

<a id="u02041"></a>

Enlightened intent transcends conceptual mind;

<a id="u02042"></a>

what has characteristics is pure in its own place;

<a id="u02043"></a>

there is liberation from the extremes of the empty and entities;

<a id="u02044"></a>

movement is exhausted, and conceptual thought is absent;

<a id="u02045"></a>

conceptual mind is exhausted, and the mental faculty is transcended. [N-091](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-091)

<a id="u02046"></a>

**[Source heading: Forty-eighth reply.]**

<a id="u02047"></a>

Ema! In the ultimate Secret Mantra,

<a id="u02048"></a>

although the stages of accomplishment are numerous,

<a id="u02049"></a>

their principal distinction is into

<a id="u02050"></a>

methods and discerning knowing, supremely profound.

<a id="u02051"></a>

Condensing [the account] from those,

<a id="u02052"></a>

for now, discerning knowing will be explained.

<a id="u02053"></a>

Yoginī, who reveals blessings:

<a id="u02054"></a>

from this there are seven practices of accomplishment.

<a id="u02055"></a>

Approach, accomplishment, and the culmination of activities—

<a id="u02056"></a>

their respective supreme mantras are supremely profound.

<a id="u02057"></a>

Through the particular combinations and connections of letters,

<a id="u02058"></a>

take the respective root mantras,

<a id="u02059"></a>

and the practitioner begins recitation. [N-092](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-092)

<a id="u02060"></a>

From the clarifiers that generate,

<a id="u02061"></a>

through their respective colors, from outside,

<a id="u02062"></a>

complete all the branches of the rite,

<a id="u02063"></a>

and arise in the form of the Blessed Lady.

<a id="u02064"></a>

Adorned with empowerment of primordial knowing,

<a id="u02065"></a>

one desiring accomplishment continually cultivates.

<a id="u02066"></a>

Focus on the garlands of āli and kāli,

<a id="u02067"></a>

and let the yogin's conceptual mind abide.

<a id="u02068"></a>

Apply the seal; rely on completion in ordinary mind.

<a id="u02069"></a>

Restore [this]; through the body of primordial knowing,

<a id="u02070"></a>

repeatedly engaging in familiarity,

<a id="u02071"></a>

with six, seven, nine, one,

<a id="u02072"></a>

two, three, and five,

<a id="u02073"></a>

there is no doubt of accomplishment. [N-092](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-092)

<a id="u02074"></a>

The Ray-Bearing One, who reveals accomplishments:

<a id="u02075"></a>

for her, there are three practices of accomplishment.

<a id="u02076"></a>

Through the inner combinations of garlands of clarifiers,

<a id="u02077"></a>

one knows these respective mantras, too.

<a id="u02078"></a>

Their approach and accomplishment are as before.

<a id="u02079"></a>

From the rite whose branches are gathered,

<a id="u02080"></a>

the goddess's color, faces, and hands—[^G-U02080]

<a id="u02081"></a>

the yogin lets the body abide clearly [as these].

<a id="u02082"></a>

From the garland of light of awakened mind,

<a id="u02083"></a>

make supplication for whatever object of focus is desired.

<a id="u02084"></a>

For ordinary mind, the letters are exceedingly clear;

<a id="u02085"></a>

leave [it] in the Great Perfection of union.

<a id="u02086"></a>

Continually cultivating in this way,

<a id="u02087"></a>

or relying continuously on six sessions,

<a id="u02088"></a>

with nine, seven, and five,

<a id="u02089"></a>

there is definitely no doubt of accomplishment. [N-092](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-092)

<a id="u02090"></a>

Tsantika, who reveals activities:

<a id="u02091"></a>

for her, too, there are three practices of accomplishment.

<a id="u02092"></a>

Through wondrous combinations of the letters of basic space,

<a id="u02093"></a>

one should know these mantras, too.

<a id="u02094"></a>

The yogin endowed with the rite

<a id="u02095"></a>

imagines one's own ordinary mind abiding pure.

<a id="u02096"></a>

In the three colors of the goddess's form,

<a id="u02097"></a>

through the application of placing letters,

<a id="u02098"></a>

one clears away discordant characteristics.

<a id="u02099"></a>

For the activities, cultivate the respective wrathful female [forms].

<a id="u02100"></a>

When the activities are complete, the yoga

<a id="u02101"></a>

of empty ordinary mind is perfected.

<a id="u02102"></a>

Through continually cultivating in this way,

<a id="u02103"></a>

with one, two, and three,

<a id="u02104"></a>

all desired purposes are accomplished. [N-092](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-092)

<a id="u02105"></a>

The profound practices of accomplishment of methods

<a id="u02106"></a>

will be explained in brief.

<a id="u02107"></a>

The culmination of activities is the Slayer of Death.

<a id="u02108"></a>

For him, there are two practices of accomplishment.

<a id="u02109"></a>

Through joining āli and kāli equally,

<a id="u02110"></a>

one should also know his mantras.

<a id="u02111"></a>

Having completed the activities of the wheel,

<a id="u02112"></a>

and with the enlightened intent of the rite complete,

<a id="u02113"></a>

Whichever letters accord with one's aspiration—

<a id="u02114"></a>

definitely imagine their colors, [N-093](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-093)

<a id="u02115"></a>

and undertake the yoga of the Slayer of Death.

<a id="u02116"></a>

In one, transform the culmination of activities;

<a id="u02117"></a>

rely on aspiration in accordance with whatever is desired.

<a id="u02118"></a>

In the other, with the letters complete,

<a id="u02119"></a>

the yogin's ordinary mind is to abide.

<a id="u02120"></a>

The culmination of power is the Unmoving Protector.

<a id="u02121"></a>

For him, there are three practices of accomplishment.

<a id="u02122"></a>

Through the respective connections of āli and kāli,

<a id="u02123"></a>

one should know these mantras.

<a id="u02124"></a>

For the five pledges to be guarded,

<a id="u02125"></a>

complete the rites.

<a id="u02126"></a>

The embodiment of the wrathful king, the Unmoving Protector—

<a id="u02127"></a>

with faith, definitely imagine one's own body [as this].

<a id="u02128"></a>

Through rays of light in the form of hooks,

<a id="u02129"></a>

proliferate the activities of summoning, killing, and binding, [N-093](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-093)[^G-U02129]

<a id="u02130"></a>

in whatever way one desires.

<a id="u02131"></a>

Dedicate [them] toward complete awakening.

<a id="u02132"></a>

For these profound practices of accomplishment:

<a id="u02133"></a>

the Slayer of Death—three hundred thousand;

<a id="u02134"></a>

with two and five [hundred thousand], yoga is complete.

<a id="u02135"></a>

The Unmoving One—one hundred thousand;

<a id="u02136"></a>

likewise, with two and three [hundred thousand], it is accomplished. [N-093](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-093)

<a id="u02137"></a>

The two practices of accomplishment of the profound meaning

<a id="u02138"></a>

are definitely condensed in brief.

<a id="u02139"></a>

Whoever entertains doubt about these

<a id="u02140"></a>

breaks the command of the buddha himself.

<a id="u02141"></a>

Therefore, accomplishment is certain. [N-093](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-093)

<a id="u02142"></a>

**[Source heading: Forty-ninth reply.]**

<a id="u02143"></a>

The manner in which the natural state abides is like this.

<a id="u02144"></a>

Were it explained, hearers and solitary buddhas

<a id="u02145"></a>

would be greatly terrified and faint.

<a id="u02146"></a>

Therefore, it should be kept supremely secret.

<a id="u02147"></a>

Phenomena without examination

<a id="u02148"></a>

are called the Ground without arising. [N-094](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-094)

<a id="u02149"></a>

Because there is no abiding in it,

<a id="u02150"></a>

neither entities nor the empty abide.

<a id="u02151"></a>

Clarity and darkness have no possible arising,

<a id="u02152"></a>

because there is no possible limit of going or coming.

<a id="u02153"></a>

Not bound by endeavor and effort,

<a id="u02154"></a>

there is openness itself, and penetration occurs. [N-094](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-094)

<a id="u02155"></a>

Because there is no confinement or falling to a side,

<a id="u02156"></a>

neither virtue nor misdeeds are established,

<a id="u02157"></a>

for both virtue and misdeeds are excluded.

<a id="u02158"></a>

Because samsara and nirvana do not abide,

<a id="u02159"></a>

and the empty nature of phenomena pervades all,

<a id="u02160"></a>

primordial knowing abides in its own identity;

<a id="u02161"></a>

in the manner of three, [they are] indivisible. [N-094](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-094)

<a id="u02162"></a>

**[Source heading: Fiftieth reply.]**

<a id="u02163"></a>

Not explained elsewhere,

<a id="u02164"></a>

the wondrous application of calculating delusion—

<a id="u02165"></a>

I will explain it; listen well.

<a id="u02166"></a>

From the root dependent connections,

<a id="u02167"></a>

with the elements of the respective bodies

<a id="u02168"></a>

and the linkages of the wheel of dependent connections,

<a id="u02169"></a>

definitely grasp the measures of the times of delusion.

<a id="u02170"></a>

From the times of coarse abiding,

<a id="u02171"></a>

identify the delusion of the yogin's ordinary mind.

<a id="u02172"></a>

The time of delusion, time of abiding, and time of reversal:[^G-U02172]

<a id="u02173"></a>

from these three distinctions, twelve measures.

<a id="u02174"></a>

Through the bounded measuring mechanism,

<a id="u02175"></a>

the skilled person grasps the cells of the grid. [N-095](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-095)

<a id="u02176"></a>

Through time, number, and appropriation,

<a id="u02177"></a>

relate [these] to aggregates, elements, and so forth.

<a id="u02178"></a>

Through the crucial point that reverses, too,

<a id="u02179"></a>

reverse the delusion of the respective embodied beings.

<a id="u02180"></a>

**[Source heading: Fifty-first reply.]**

<a id="u02181"></a>

Regarding the individual characteristics of objects of knowledge,

<a id="u02182"></a>

through knowing the transformations of general and particular,

<a id="u02183"></a>

although the respective assertions differ,[^G-U02183]

<a id="u02184"></a>

for viewing the worldly activity of the mental faculty,

<a id="u02185"></a>

conceptual minds delighting in elaborations

<a id="u02186"></a>

temporarily engage with words. [N-096](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-096)

<a id="u02187"></a>

In the tradition, through two particular combinations,

<a id="a2000-c01-s04"></a>

*Scan-restored main verses:*[^G-A2000-C01-S04]

place those with perception at the limit.<br />
Through pith instructions on the crucial point of direct perception,

<a id="u02188"></a>

cut the elaborations that distinguish samsara and nirvana.

<a id="u02189"></a>

Through the wheel of the crucial points of cultivation,

<a id="u02190"></a>

cut the elaborations of the connection of wind-mind.

<a id="u02191"></a>

Through the sequence of the path of entering knowing,

<a id="u02192"></a>

cut the conceptual minds of doubt and delusion. [N-096](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-096)

<a id="u02193"></a>

**[Source heading: Fifty-second reply.]**

<a id="u02194"></a>

By the guru, the great vajra-holder,

<a id="u02195"></a>

the sequence will be explained.

<a id="u02196"></a>

The guru whose characteristics are complete

<a id="u02197"></a>

is the source of all qualities

<a id="u02198"></a>

and upholds the Ground of complete awakening. [N-097](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-097)

<a id="u02199"></a>

There are two gurus: root and branch.

<a id="u02200"></a>

For those whose respective characteristics are complete,

<a id="u02201"></a>

with sovereignty and one's own body,

<a id="u02202"></a>

retinue, and enjoyments, rely on [them].

<a id="u02203"></a>

Pleasing [them] through bodily and verbal activity,

<a id="u02204"></a>

rely on the guru in equal union with the buddha. [N-097](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-097)

<a id="u02205"></a>

Endowed with faith and discerning knowing,

<a id="u02206"></a>

with aspiration and without delusion,

<a id="u02207"></a>

free from delusion and error about what is to be expressed,

<a id="u02208"></a>

with clear faculties, rely on the guru.

<a id="u02209"></a>

From this reliance, the qualities [attained]

<a id="u02210"></a>

are like a wish-granting tree, a wish-fulfilling jewel,

<a id="u02211"></a>

and a cow that yields whatever is desired:

<a id="u02212"></a>

one attains immeasurable qualities.

<a id="u02213"></a>

Focusing on that, rely on the guru,

<a id="u02214"></a>

for it turns one back from the battlefield of samsara. [N-097](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-097)

<a id="u02215"></a>

**[Source heading: Fifty-third reply.]**

<a id="u02216"></a>

The outer elemental signs are solidity,

<a id="u02217"></a>

heat and burning, gathering and moisture,

<a id="u02218"></a>

lightness and separation, and pervasion.

<a id="u02219"></a>

Through portions of what is to be learned about the elements,

<a id="u02220"></a>

the yogin's body and speech

<a id="u02221"></a>

hold these as well.

<a id="u02222"></a>

The definite times of the twelve years,

<a id="u02223"></a>

properly joined in accord with oneself:

<a id="u02224"></a>

at the beginning of each respective time,

<a id="u02225"></a>

sound, instructions, and so forth—[N-098](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-098)[^G-U02225]

<a id="u02226"></a>

from twelve magical displays of objects of focus,

<a id="u02227"></a>

the gods—among the worldly gods,

<a id="u02228"></a>
