the eleven who act as protectors—

<a id="u02229"></a>

are definitely taken as objects of focus as portents,

<a id="u02230"></a>

and made to appear in Jambudvīpa. [N-098](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-098)

<a id="u02231"></a>

Through their manner of vanishing into space,

<a id="u02232"></a>

the magical displays, too, are definitely distinguished.

<a id="u02233"></a>

Grasping the directions of one's own place in Jambudvīpa,

<a id="u02234"></a>

to the kings of the east and so forth,

<a id="u02235"></a>

great people, common people, and others,

<a id="u02236"></a>

they definitely show virtue and nonvirtue.

<a id="u02237"></a>

Likewise, the success and failure of kingdoms,

<a id="u02238"></a>

virtue and nonvirtue, are examined by the skilled person.

<a id="u02239"></a>

One thereby gains accomplishment concerning entities,

<a id="u02240"></a>

and worldly higher knowing is definitely accomplished. [N-098](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-098)

<a id="u02241"></a>

**[Source heading: Fifty-fourth reply.]**

<a id="u02242"></a>

The time is when the continuation of karma awakens,

<a id="u02243"></a>

when the elements transform, and at the crucial point of ordinary mind.

<a id="u02244"></a>

Through purification of delusion, one's own time is found.

<a id="u02245"></a>

That is the time of its reversal.

<a id="u02246"></a>

The teaching's hearth (thab) is the empty,

<a id="u02247"></a>

emptiness itself, and the terminator. [N-099](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-099)

<a id="u02248"></a>

The remaining times of individual persons

<a id="u02249"></a>

are the same as what was taught above.

<a id="u02250"></a>

**[Source heading: Fifty-fifth reply.]**

<a id="u02251"></a>

Ema! Lord of gods, sovereign,

<a id="u02252"></a>

faithful one of great fortune, listen.

<a id="u02253"></a>

Hidden in other continua,

<a id="u02254"></a>

known by no one,

<a id="u02255"></a>

the rite of conferring empowerment will be explained.

<a id="u02256"></a>

Outer, inner, secret,

<a id="u02257"></a>

and the fully complete secret:

<a id="u02258"></a>

through these four combinations,

<a id="u02259"></a>

the four—beings' bodies, speech, mental faculty, and ordinary mind—

<a id="u02260"></a>

in order to purify [them],

<a id="u02261"></a>

through four empowerments, too,

<a id="u02262"></a>

bring the fortunate to maturity. [N-100](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-100)

<a id="u02263"></a>

With elaborations, without elaborations,

<a id="u02264"></a>

utterly without elaborations,

<a id="u02265"></a>

and, likewise, completely without elaborations:

<a id="u02266"></a>

through these four distinctions,

<a id="u02267"></a>

the faithful mature their own continua.

<a id="u02268"></a>

For the purpose of [empowerment] with elaborations, too,

<a id="u02269"></a>

approach, accomplishment, and close approach

<a id="u02270"></a>

are to be undertaken by a guru whose characteristics are complete.

<a id="u02271"></a>

Furthermore, with the earth rite,

<a id="u02272"></a>

preparations, lines, and spreading colors,

<a id="u02273"></a>

construct the maṇḍala that occurs in the continuum.

<a id="u02274"></a>

Then, for the rite of conferring empowerment, too,

<a id="u02275"></a>

beginning with the hearer vehicle,

<a id="u02276"></a>

complete the eight stages,

<a id="u02277"></a>

then confer the Great Perfection empowerment of awareness's expressiveness. [N-100](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-100)

<a id="u02278"></a>

Explain their respective enlightened intents,

<a id="u02279"></a>

entry, and distinctive capacities.

<a id="u02280"></a>

To mature the faithful who enter without elaborations,

<a id="u02281"></a>

for the supreme empowerment without elaborations,

<a id="u02282"></a>

relying on a maṇḍala and vase,

<a id="u02283"></a>

give empowerment in verses.

<a id="u02284"></a>

For [empowerment] utterly without elaborations,

<a id="u02285"></a>

distinguish samsara and nirvana through the secret preliminary, as below. [N-100](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-100)

<a id="u02286"></a>

Then, through the characteristics of space,

<a id="u02287"></a>

abandon the obscurations of body, speech, and mental faculty.

<a id="u02288"></a>

Definitely examine [this] through its characteristics.

<a id="u02289"></a>

When activity is impure,

<a id="u02290"></a>

purify [it] through virtue appropriate to each individual.

<a id="u02291"></a>

For empowerment, relying on signs,

<a id="u02292"></a>

introduce the own identity of clear self-awareness. [N-T46](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-t46)

<a id="u02293"></a>

The skilled person should give pith instructions that explain the signs.

<a id="u02294"></a>

For [empowerment] completely without elaborations,

<a id="u02295"></a>

open the gate of the maṇḍala of ordinary mind,

<a id="u02296"></a>

and definitely undertake the bodily posture.

<a id="u02297"></a>

Becoming familiar with it, apply the gaze.

<a id="u02298"></a>

Directly perceived awareness is the sign of authoritative transmission. [N-100](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-100)

<a id="u02299"></a>

**[Source heading: Fifty-sixth reply.]**

<a id="u02300"></a>

Hero Free from Conceptualization, son of the gods,

<a id="u02301"></a>

to satisfy the sovereign Delight-Maker,

<a id="u02302"></a>

the pledges depending on empowerment will be explained.

<a id="u02303"></a>

Though words do not suffice for their particulars,

<a id="u02304"></a>

in brief, relate embodiment, speech, and awakened mind

<a id="u02305"></a>

to the yogin's body, speech, and mental faculty.

<a id="u02306"></a>

The objects of focus—the guru and vajra siblings—

<a id="u02307"></a>

are distinguished as the objects to be guarded.

<a id="u02308"></a>

Vows are included in body and speech. [N-101](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-101)

<a id="a2000-c01-s05"></a>

*Scan-restored main verses:*[^G-A2000-C01-S05]

The site of the vajra secret is to be held.

<a id="a2000-c01-s06"></a>

**[Source heading: Fifty-seventh reply.]**[^G-A2000-C01-S06]

<a id="a2000-c01-s07"></a>

*Scan-restored main verses:*[^G-A2000-C01-S07]

Again, Lord of Gods, Joy-Maker, listen.

<a id="u02309"></a>

Through verbal expressions and the categories of tenet systems,

<a id="u02310"></a>

the nine stages involve the activity of the mental faculty.

<a id="u02311"></a>

Separated from the crucial point of direct perception,

<a id="u02312"></a>

future sentient beings to be trained

<a id="u02313"></a>

will place their trust in words.

<a id="u02314"></a>

Sentient beings with coarse conceptual thought,

<a id="u02315"></a>

because they engage in words, are not mature.

<a id="u02316"></a>

At the final time of this teaching,

<a id="u02317"></a>

this is called the ground of aspirational activity. [N-101](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-101)

<a id="u02318"></a>

**[Source heading: Fifty-eighth reply.]**

<a id="u02319"></a>

Beyond words, elaborations are greatly complete. [N-102](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-102)

<a id="u02320"></a>

This continuum of definite secrecy,

<a id="u02321"></a>

relying on the path of direct perception,

<a id="u02322"></a>

gathers one's own knowing into awareness.

<a id="u02323"></a>

Gathering all buddhas,

<a id="u02324"></a>

the definite nature of phenomena of self-awareness

<a id="u02325"></a>

is enacted through evenness without mixing. [N-102](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-102)

<a id="u02326"></a>

This is the stage beyond words.

<a id="u02327"></a>

**[Source heading: Fifty-ninth reply.]**

<a id="u02328"></a>

The crucial point of direct perception: body, speech, and ordinary mind.

<a id="u02329"></a>

Definitely bring their respective crucial points into accord.

<a id="u02330"></a>

Furthermore, the body [abides] in the three embodiments;

<a id="u02331"></a>

one should never depart from them. [N-103](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-103)

<a id="u02332"></a>

For speech, training and abiding, [N-103](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-103)[^G-U02332]

<a id="u02333"></a>

and likewise passing beyond, are to be taught.

<a id="u02334"></a>

Ordinary mind is accomplished by directing it toward basic space.

<a id="u02335"></a>

Do not separate from the pith instructions on the three crucial points.

<a id="u02336"></a>

Furthermore, rely on the crucial points of the door, object,

<a id="u02337"></a>

wind, and awareness.

<a id="u02338"></a>

For the door, view the three embodiments with the eyes.

<a id="u02339"></a>

For the object, be free of what obscures.

<a id="u02340"></a>

Through wind and awareness being slow, primordial knowing is accomplished.

<a id="u02341"></a>

In this there are four visions.

<a id="u02342"></a>

Through the vision of the direct perception of the nature of phenomena,

<a id="u02343"></a>

one transcends words held through mental examination. [N-103](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-103)

<a id="u02344"></a>

Through the vision of increasing experience,

<a id="u02345"></a>

delusive appearances are made to subside.

<a id="u02346"></a>

Through the vision of awareness reaching its limit,

<a id="u02347"></a>

one transcends the path appearances of realizing the three embodiments.

<a id="u02348"></a>

Through the vision of the exhaustion of nature of phenomena,

<a id="u02349"></a>

one cuts the flow of samsara of the three realms.

<a id="u02350"></a>

Rely on this to the end and have conviction. [N-103](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-103)

<a id="u02351"></a>

With a Ground in the three nonmovements,

<a id="u02352"></a>

one arrives at the crucial point of wind-mind.

<a id="u02353"></a>

Taking the measure through three ways of remaining,

<a id="u02354"></a>

dreams are reversed; for body, speech, and mental faculty,

<a id="u02355"></a>

signs and measures are definitely grasped.

<a id="u02356"></a>

Fixing [this] with the three attainments,

<a id="u02357"></a>

contaminated aggregates do not appear. [N-103](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-103)

<a id="u02358"></a>

**[Source heading: Sixtieth reply.]**

<a id="u02359"></a>

Ema! How greatly wondrous!

<a id="u02360"></a>

Unless samsara and nirvana are distinguished through the secret preliminary,

<a id="u02361"></a>

the connection of the three realms with body, speech,

<a id="u02362"></a>

and mental faculty will not be cut.

<a id="u02363"></a>

Therefore, I explain distinguishing samsara and nirvana through the secret preliminary.

<a id="u02364"></a>

This has three aspects. [N-104](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-104)

<a id="u02365"></a>

For the body: walking and remaining,

<a id="u02366"></a>

compressing [the body] and manifold bodily mechanisms,

<a id="u02367"></a>

extending and gathering the limbs,

<a id="u02368"></a>

and relying on manifold actions.

<a id="u02369"></a>

Rousing [these] with a mindful ordinary mind,

<a id="u02370"></a>

the activities of the forms of the six destinies—

<a id="u02371"></a>

take [them] up in ordinary mind and perform [them] with the body.

<a id="u02372"></a>

Whoever can engage in this way—

<a id="u02373"></a>

whatever is undertaken, its flow is later cut. [N-104](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-104)

<a id="u02374"></a>

This has two purposes,

<a id="u02375"></a>

examined as common and supreme.

<a id="u02376"></a>

When signs and measures are reached in this way,

<a id="u02377"></a>

the yogin begins verbal activity.

<a id="u02378"></a>

Because expression has the intrinsic nature of conceptual thought,

<a id="u02379"></a>

[use] manifold sounds and voices.

<a id="u02380"></a>

Because whatever is spoken has no reversal, [N-104](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-104)

<a id="u02381"></a>

[use] the voices of gods, nāgas, and yakṣas,

<a id="u02382"></a>

gandharvas, kumbhāṇḍas, and the All-Pervader;

<a id="u02383"></a>

utter their favorable and unfavorable varieties.

<a id="u02384"></a>

In brief, the voices of the six destinies:

<a id="u02385"></a>

utter ordinary mind's mindfulness and reflection through speech. [N-104](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-104)

<a id="u02386"></a>

Here, too, there are two purposes:

<a id="u02387"></a>

the distinction between supreme and common culminations.

<a id="u02388"></a>

Having reached the measure in this way, too,

<a id="u02389"></a>

the yogin should train ordinary mind.

<a id="u02390"></a>

For past, future, and present,

<a id="u02391"></a>

rely on the wheel of mindfulness and reflection, proliferating and gathering.

<a id="u02392"></a>

Ordinary minds of liking and disliking, favorable and unfavorable—

<a id="u02393"></a>

all manifold [notions] of existence and nonexistence—

<a id="u02394"></a>

examine outwardly and reflect inwardly.

<a id="u02395"></a>

The mindfulness of whatever is reflected on—

<a id="u02396"></a>

afterward, its flow of arising will definitely be cut. [N-104](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-104)

<a id="u02397"></a>

Its purposes are distinguished by what was said above.

<a id="u02398"></a>

When activity has been completed in this way,

<a id="u02399"></a>

continually apply [it] to the crucial point of direct perception.

<a id="u02400"></a>

When that is complete, one does not enter the three realms.

<a id="u02401"></a>

It should be taught in utmost secrecy.

<a id="u02402"></a>

**[Source heading: Sixty-first reply.]**

<a id="u02403"></a>

Relying on bodily activity,

<a id="u02404"></a>

one wishing to retain the inner wind:

<a id="u02405"></a>

the twenty-one activities of retaining wind— [N-105](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-105)

<a id="u02406"></a>

when the yogin performs and engages in these,

<a id="u02407"></a>

one becomes familiar with wind and proficient in the body.

<a id="u02408"></a>

For one endowed with ornaments,

<a id="u02409"></a>

make a wheel of dependent connections at the place.

<a id="u02410"></a>

Direct wind and knowing toward the ornaments.

<a id="u02411"></a>

While moving, apply the manner of gazing with the eyes.

<a id="u02412"></a>

While remaining, project ordinary mind. [N-105](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-105)

<a id="u02413"></a>

**[Source heading: Sixty-second reply.]**

<a id="u02414"></a>

Through twenty-one trainings in retaining ordinary mind,

<a id="u02415"></a>

first, bring it to abide;

<a id="u02416"></a>

second, separate body and ordinary mind;

<a id="u02417"></a>

third, see the nature of phenomena.

<a id="u02418"></a>

At this time, the crucial points of body, speech, and ordinary mind

<a id="u02419"></a>

are to be allowed to settle by one endowed with yoga.

<a id="u02420"></a>

Also with the signs of the wind that causes movement,

<a id="u02421"></a>

mixing, projecting, and gathering—

<a id="u02422"></a>

when the skilled person relies on these crucial points,

<a id="u02423"></a>

the flow of ordinary mind's conceptual thought is cut. [N-105](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-105)

<a id="u02424"></a>

**[Source heading: Sixty-third reply.]**

<a id="u02425"></a>

There are twenty-one signs of death, too.

<a id="u02426"></a>

Having gone to a secluded charnel-ground place,

<a id="u02427"></a>

offer to the guru and make a torma.

<a id="u02428"></a>

Furthermore, with food and drink,

<a id="u02429"></a>

satisfy other beings as well.

<a id="u02430"></a>

Then examine the outer, inner, and secret

<a id="u02431"></a>

signs of death, too.

<a id="u02432"></a>

When they are unfavorable, undertake reversal;

<a id="u02433"></a>

when favorable, make supplication to the guru. [N-105](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-105)

<a id="u02434"></a>

**[Source heading: Sixty-fourth reply.]**

<a id="u02435"></a>

Lord of the gods, for the sake of sentient beings,

<a id="u02436"></a>

the answer to that compassionate question you asked—

<a id="u02437"></a>

I teach it; grasp it without delusion.

<a id="u02438"></a>

In the Great Perfection of intrinsic nature,

<a id="u02439"></a>

although the conceptual mind of a view with a directed object of focus is exhausted,

<a id="u02440"></a>

for now, establish certainty through words. [N-106](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-106)

<a id="u02441"></a>

Through the crucial point of directly perceiving the nature of phenomena, too,

<a id="u02442"></a>

one realizes that activities have neither favorable nor unfavorable [status].

<a id="u02443"></a>

Through definitely gathering the heart-sphere,

<a id="u02444"></a>

faculties are neither sharp nor dull.

<a id="u02445"></a>

Because the limit of the words of mental examination is exhausted,

<a id="u02446"></a>

one sees through the faculty of the eye of discerning knowing.

<a id="u02447"></a>

Because words do not reveal the nature of phenomena,

<a id="u02448"></a>

the common vehicles do not accomplish the meaning.

<a id="u02449"></a>

Because the three embodiments are path appearances,

<a id="u02450"></a>

seeing this, one does not return to the three realms. [N-106](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-106)

<a id="u02451"></a>

Because all phenomena are equally of one taste,

<a id="u02452"></a>

there is no dependence on Ground and result.

<a id="u02453"></a>

Because self-awareness is free from all conceptual thought,

<a id="u02454"></a>

there are four ways of liberation.

<a id="u02455"></a>

Through confidence in liberation at the four crucial points,

<a id="u02456"></a>

samsara and passing beyond sorrow do not abide.

<a id="u02457"></a>

Phenomena that have not arisen and are not made

<a id="u02458"></a>

have neither Ground nor root.

<a id="u02459"></a>

The crucial points of liberation through hearing and seeing

<a id="u02460"></a>

are realized through two, five, and eight. [N-106](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-106)

<a id="u02461"></a>

Through four great confidences, one joins basic space.

<a id="u02462"></a>

Through three instances of pointing out, one should gain conviction.

<a id="u02463"></a>

**[Source heading: Sixty-fifth reply.]**

<a id="u02464"></a>

Next, the life-tree of cultivation is explained.

<a id="u02465"></a>

Definitely relate [this] through the authoritative transmission of ordinary mind.

<a id="u02466"></a>

Furthermore, concerning the crucial points of cultivation,

<a id="u02467"></a>

preliminary practice, main practice,

<a id="u02468"></a>

and subsequent practice are held to be three.

<a id="u02469"></a>

Training the body, taming the nature of ordinary mind,

<a id="u02470"></a>

and completing the activities of speech,

<a id="u02471"></a>

the nature of ordinary mind enters the path.

<a id="u02472"></a>

Relying on wind, body and ordinary mind separate.

<a id="u02473"></a>

In order to cut the opening from which the three realms proliferate,

<a id="u02474"></a>

reverse attachment to body and speech from its depths.

<a id="u02475"></a>

Because the precipices of the six destinies are equal,

<a id="u02476"></a>

press the crucial points of body, speech, and mental faculty. [N-107](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-107)

<a id="u02477"></a>

In order to mix the nature of ordinary mind with phenomena,

<a id="u02478"></a>

draw wind and knowing upward.

<a id="u02479"></a>

In order to exhaust delusive appearances,

<a id="u02480"></a>

continually become familiar with wind. [N-107](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-107)

<a id="u02481"></a>

**[Source heading: Sixty-sixth reply.]**

<a id="u02482"></a>

For the application of activity, in basic space and awareness,

<a id="u02483"></a>

endowed with three fixings, without separation,

<a id="u02484"></a>

continual familiarity makes activity easeful.[^G-U02484]

<a id="u02485"></a>

When all phenomena are free from doing,

<a id="u02486"></a>

and the connection of samsara and nirvana is cut,

<a id="u02487"></a>

delusion with conceptual thought is stopped.

<a id="u02488"></a>

By cutting the flow of samsara's precipice,

<a id="u02489"></a>

of the great yogin's activity—[N-108](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-108)[^G-U02489]

<a id="scan-ch1-layer-02489"></a>

**[Source heading: Sixty-seventh reply.]**[^G-SCAN-CH1-LAYER-02489]

<a id="u02490"></a>

although the result cannot be expressed,

<a id="u02491"></a>

one reaches the exhaustion of phenomena,

<a id="u02492"></a>

and the limits of one's own tenet system, too, collapse.

<a id="u02493"></a>

At this time, the guru's pith instructions subside.

<a id="u02494"></a>

Because the limits of viewing, cultivation, and activity are empty,

<a id="u02495"></a>

there is no appearance as phenomena.

<a id="u02496"></a>

Because the flow of embodiments and primordial knowing is cut,

<a id="u02497"></a>

there are neither buddhas nor sentient beings.

<a id="u02498"></a>

In brief, nothing whatsoever abides.

<a id="u02499"></a>

Because there is no going, there is no coming. [N-108](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-108)

<a id="u02500"></a>

**[Source heading: Sixty-eighth reply.]**

<a id="u02501"></a>

One wishing to cut the flow of samsara,

<a id="u02502"></a>

having reversed attachment to body and speech,

<a id="u02503"></a>

should press the crucial point of direct perception.

<a id="u02504"></a>

Through the application of calculating delusion,

<a id="u02505"></a>

grasp the measures of the times of samsara and nirvana,

<a id="u02506"></a>

and become skilled in the crucial point of reversal.

<a id="u02507"></a>

By reaching the measure of the six capabilities,

<a id="u02508"></a>

one is thoroughly liberated from the six faults.

<a id="u02509"></a>

This cuts the flow of samsara. [N-109](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-109)

<a id="u02510"></a>

**[Source heading: Sixty-ninth reply.]**

<a id="u02511"></a>

Taking nourishment from vital essences is like this:

<a id="u02512"></a>

not taught in any other continuum,

<a id="u02513"></a>

Ema! It is explained as greatly wondrous.

<a id="u02514"></a>

Lord of gods, hero, listen!

<a id="u02515"></a>

By relying on precious substances,

<a id="u02516"></a>

the lifespan becomes equal to the sun and moon.

<a id="u02517"></a>

Through preparations of various stones,

<a id="u02518"></a>

weapons do not penetrate the body.

<a id="u02519"></a>

Through preparations relying on herbs,

<a id="u02520"></a>

white hair and wrinkles disappear. [N-110](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-110)

<a id="u02521"></a>

Through taking nourishment from vital essences of medicinal preparations,

<a id="u02522"></a>

one becomes endowed with splendor and youthful.[^G-U02522]

<a id="u02523"></a>

Through preparations relying on meat,

<a id="u02524"></a>

the bodily constituents are sustained, and strength develops.

<a id="u02525"></a>

Furthermore, relying on wind,

<a id="u02526"></a>

greatly wondrous taking of nourishment from vital essences is explained.

<a id="u02527"></a>

What is called cutting the flow of the elements

<a id="u02528"></a>

is known through the upper and lower winds.

<a id="u02529"></a>

For the skilled person, wind is easily made to abide.

<a id="u02530"></a>

Next, through calculating wind's movement,

<a id="u02531"></a>

there is the yoga of food.

<a id="u02532"></a>

Relying on the elements,

<a id="u02533"></a>

whatever appears becomes food.

<a id="u02534"></a>

Through taking nourishment from vital essences in this way,

<a id="u02535"></a>

the torments of beings are cut off. [N-110](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-110)

<a id="u02536"></a>

**[Source heading: Seventieth reply.]**

<a id="u02537"></a>

The yoga of clothing is like this:

<a id="u02538"></a>

perform it through wind and profound dependent connections.

<a id="u02539"></a>

From projecting and gathering wind,

<a id="u02540"></a>

through the crucial point of going, coming, and holding, too,

<a id="u02541"></a>

distinguish the respective elemental objects of focus

<a id="u02542"></a>

in accord with the yogin's body.

<a id="u02543"></a>

Through the application of dependent connections,

<a id="u02544"></a>

with two, six, seven, and three,

<a id="u02545"></a>

properly combine pills and powders,

<a id="u02546"></a>

and carry out the culmination of ingesting and applying [them]. [N-111](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-111)

<a id="u02547"></a>

**[Source heading: Seventy-first reply.]**

<a id="u02548"></a>

Next, for cutting the flow of the body's elements,

<a id="u02549"></a>

greatly wondrous taking of nourishment from vital essences is explained.

<a id="u02550"></a>

By continually circulating and reversing ‘great odor’ (dri chen), [N-112](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-112)

<a id="u02551"></a>

finally, the body's discharges are exhausted.

<a id="u02552"></a>

Alternatively, extracted vital essence and liquid butter—

<a id="u02553"></a>

by relying on [these] in the proper measure,

<a id="u02554"></a>

the flows of the elements are cut.

<a id="u02555"></a>

Because the flow of the body's elements is cut,

<a id="u02556"></a>

living creatures do not abide in the body;

<a id="u02557"></a>

there are no feces, urine, and so forth;

<a id="u02558"></a>

outer and inner wind, too, cease to be. [N-112](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-112)

<a id="u02559"></a>

At this time, the great yogin

<a id="u02560"></a>

becomes pure with respect to both food and clothing.

<a id="u02561"></a>

Through this, concerning lifespan and enjoyments,

<a id="u02562"></a>

whatever is desired is accomplished. [N-112](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-112)

<a id="u02563"></a>

**[Source heading: Seventy-second reply.]**

<a id="u02564"></a>

For the four elements,

<a id="u02565"></a>

a yogin who makes their tastes equal

<a id="u02566"></a>

brings the crucial points of earth, water, fire, and wind into accord.

<a id="u02567"></a>

Mixing [them] with the body, raise [them] through wind.

<a id="u02568"></a>

Transform one's own body through the respective elements,

<a id="u02569"></a>

and, in engaging in this itself,

<a id="u02570"></a>

cultivate the maṇḍala gathering the crucial points. [N-113](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-113)

<a id="u02571"></a>

Through reaching the measure of familiarity with this,

<a id="u02572"></a>

the powers of the four become equal in their own identity.

<a id="u02573"></a>

One engages easefully and is free from destruction.

<a id="u02574"></a>

Through this, too, both food and clothing are accomplished,

<a id="u02575"></a>

and lifespan and enjoyments are complete.

<a id="u02576"></a>

**[Source heading: Seventy-third reply.]**

<a id="u02577"></a>

The qualities of the treasury are like this:

<a id="u02578"></a>

with common and supreme accomplishments,

<a id="u02579"></a>

one plays as one pleases, accomplishing [them] easefully.

<a id="u02580"></a>

The branches of dependent connections are complete.

<a id="u02581"></a>

Miracles and higher knowing, too,

<a id="u02582"></a>

are definitely accomplished through engaging with the treasury.

<a id="u02583"></a>

Illnesses and measures of time become known. [N-113](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-113)

<a id="u02584"></a>

**[Source heading: Seventy-fourth reply.]**

<a id="u02585"></a>

One wishing to accomplish noble miracles,

<a id="u02586"></a>

with a support that is an entity or with what is not an entity,

<a id="u02587"></a>

definitely accomplishes [them] through training in wind.

<a id="u02588"></a>

Word, craft, and dependent connections;

<a id="u02589"></a>

signs, phrases, and conventions;

<a id="u02590"></a>

examples, actions, and methods of training—

<a id="u02591"></a>

relying on these and engaging in karmic wind,

<a id="u02592"></a>

one accomplishes whatever miracles are desired.

<a id="u02593"></a>

Through cultivating primordial knowing free from conceptualization,

<a id="u02594"></a>

reflecting on emptiness, and the crucial point, too,

<a id="u02595"></a>

without endeavor or effort, miracles

<a id="u02596"></a>

will definitely be accomplished. [N-114](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-114)

<a id="u02597"></a>

**[Source heading: Seventy-fifth reply.]**[^G-U02597]

<a id="u02598"></a>

One wishing to enjoy inexhaustible treasure

<a id="u02599"></a>

relies on the mechanism of the market (khrom).

<a id="u02600"></a>

Become skilled in calculating its application. [N-114](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-114)

<a id="u02601"></a>

Endowed with times of three, four, and two,

<a id="u02602"></a>

having concealed [it] for ten months and thoroughly dried [it],

<a id="u02603"></a>

begin directing [it] toward whichever direction is desired.

<a id="u02604"></a>

By joining [it] with rays and wind,

<a id="u02605"></a>

whatever activities one wishes to undertake are accomplished. [N-114](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-114)

<a id="u02606"></a>

Alternatively, through profound practice of accomplishment,

<a id="u02607"></a>

distinguishing approach, accomplishment, and the culmination of activities,

<a id="u02608"></a>

rely on the lord of wealth.

<a id="u02609"></a>

Through familiarity with mantra, enjoy [the treasure].

<a id="u02610"></a>

**[Source heading: Seventy-sixth reply.]**

<a id="u02611"></a>

By bringing four and five under one's power,

<a id="u02612"></a>

the master of ordinary mind holds the body.

<a id="u02613"></a>

Within the wheels of the three places, too,

<a id="u02614"></a>

with four and five syllables, and the assignment of forms, [N-115](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-115)

<a id="u02615"></a>

*Source annotation moved to endnotes; no root verse at U02615.*[^G-U02615]

<a id="u02616"></a>

perform proliferating, gathering, absorbing, and coiling.

<a id="u02617"></a>

Through completing this, there is flight,

<a id="u02618"></a>

entry without separation, and no burning;

<a id="u02619"></a>

one cannot sink and can run. [N-115](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-115)

<a id="u02620"></a>

*Source annotation moved to endnotes; no root verse at U02620.*[^G-U02620]

<a id="u02621"></a>

The teacher, the buddha, the Blessed One,

<a id="u02622"></a>

all his qualities complete in themselves,

<a id="u02623"></a>

without utterance or spoken discourse,

<a id="u02624"></a>

from the play of naturally arisen primordial knowing,

<a id="u02625"></a>

as a miraculous display of words, phrases, and names,

<a id="u02626"></a>

appears to those of good fortune.

<a id="u02627"></a>

Thus, the secret phrases of the Great Perfection—

<a id="u02628"></a>

the lord of the gods, having definitely heard them,

<a id="u02629"></a>

with respectful body and palms properly joined,

<a id="u02630"></a>

rejoiced in the teacher himself. [N-115](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-115)

<a id="u02631"></a>

From the Great Perfection of everything,

<a id="u02632"></a>

intrinsic nature became free from elaborations,

<a id="u02633"></a>

inseparable from the basic space of the nature of phenomena. [N-115](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-115)

<a id="u02634"></a>

Thus, from The Great All-Penetrating Word, the Root of All Phenomena,

<a id="u02635"></a>

this is the first chapter: ‘Array of Diversity: The Definite Arising of the Root of Naturally Arisen Pith Instructions.’ [N-115](../LEGACY-NOTES.md#n-115)[^G-U02635]

<a id="a2000-c01-s09"></a>

*[Unresolved source inscription at the Chapter 1/2 boundary; no transcription supplied.]*[^G-A2000-C01-S09]
